### Task 3: Branching mall between retail wings (`SYN-TYP-014`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/BranchingMall/BranchingMallGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/BranchingMall/BranchingMallGeneratorTests.cs`, `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/BranchingMallTypologyTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/BranchingMall/Snapshots/branching-mall-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: as Task 2, with `HubArmsLayout.HubWithStrips`, `SpaceType.Mall` and `Retail`, and `ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Mall` and `Retail` (S8.4).
- Produces: `public sealed record BranchingMallParameters(int BranchCount, double BranchLength, double MallWidth, double ShopDepth, double AnchorDepth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (3, 40, 8, 12, 30, 5, 0); `public sealed record BranchingMallPresets(ProgramPreset Mall, ProgramPreset Retail) : PlanPresets` (slots `Mall`, `Retail`); `public sealed class BranchingMallGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `BranchingMallGenerator`, `public const int MinimumBranches = 3`, `MaximumBranches = 4`: the hub (the central court) and every arm are `2 · ShopDepth + MallWidth` wide, each branch `BranchLength + AnchorDepth` long; `MALL` (*Mall*, `Mall`) is one polygon, the court with the mall strip of every branch (`HubWithStrips`), and each branch `i` has the shop wings `B{i}-S1` (clockwise of the mall) and `B{i}-S2` along its first `BranchLength` metres and the anchor `B{i}-AN` across its end (*Branch i Shops k*, *Branch i Anchor*, `Retail`); errors `InvalidParameter` for a branch count outside 3–4 (`BranchCount must be at least 3 (two branches are a bend, not a branching), got 2.`, `BranchCount must be at most 4 (branches leave the central court only on its four sides, D-100), got 5.`) and for each length that is not positive and finite; provenance keys `BranchCount`, `BranchLength`, `MallWidth`, `ShopDepth`, `AnchorDepth`. By default a T of 7744 m²: the mall 1984 m², six shop wings of 480 m², and three anchors of 960 m²; 16 windows with 806 m² of glazing, the mall's only window on the court's south side; with four branches the mall has no outdoor wall.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/BranchingMall/BranchingMallGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

25 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (zone IDs, names, space types, areas); the mall as one branching polygon through the court and every branch; tiling for four sizes and counts; every shop wing and anchor adjoining the mall (3 and 4 branches); the mall's south entrance with three branches and no outdoor wall with four; the window rule; two branch counts outside 3–4; five invalid lengths; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.BranchingMall;

public sealed class BranchingMallGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly BranchingMallPresets Examples = new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Mall, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Retail);

    private static readonly string[] CanonicalZones =
    {
        "MALL", "B1-S1", "B1-S2", "B1-AN", "B2-S1", "B2-S2", "B2-AN", "B3-S1", "B3-S2", "B3-AN",
    };

    private readonly BranchingMallGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new BranchingMallParameters(BranchCount: 3, BranchLength: 40.0, MallWidth: 8.0, ShopDepth: 12.0, AnchorDepth: 30.0, FloorHeight: 5.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            BranchingMallParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsAMallThroughThreeBranchesOfShopsAndAnchors()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(CanonicalZones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "Mall", "Branch 1 Shops 1", "Branch 1 Shops 2", "Branch 1 Anchor", "Branch 2 Shops 1", "Branch 2 Shops 2", "Branch 2 Anchor",
                "Branch 3 Shops 1", "Branch 3 Shops 2", "Branch 3 Anchor",
            },
            plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Mall, plan.Zones[0].SpaceType);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Skip(1), z => Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Retail, z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(7744.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal((32.0 * 32.0) + (3 * 40.0 * 8.0), plan.Zones[0].FloorArea, 9);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Name.Contains("Shops")), z => Assert.Equal(480.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Name.EndsWith("Anchor")), z => Assert.Equal(960.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(102, 0, 142, 12), Outer(plan, "B1-S1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(102, 20, 142, 32), Outer(plan, "B1-S2"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(142, 0, 172, 32), Outer(plan, "B1-AN"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(90, 32, 102, 72), Outer(plan, "B2-S1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(70, 72, 102, 102), Outer(plan, "B2-AN"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 20, 70, 32), Outer(plan, "B3-S1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(0, 0, 30, 32), Outer(plan, "B3-AN"));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(5.0, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheMallIsOneBranchingPolygonThroughTheHubAndEveryBranch()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default);
        Zone mall = plan.Zones[0];

        Assert.Single(mall.Parts);
        Assert.Empty(mall.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                new Point2(70, 0), new Point2(102, 0), new Point2(102, 12), new Point2(142, 12), new Point2(142, 20), new Point2(102, 20),
                new Point2(102, 32), new Point2(90, 32), new Point2(90, 72), new Point2(82, 72), new Point2(82, 32), new Point2(70, 32),
                new Point2(70, 20), new Point2(30, 20), new Point2(30, 12), new Point2(70, 12),
            },
            mall.Parts[0].Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(172, 0), new Point2(172, 32), new Point2(102, 32), new Point2(102, 102), new Point2(70, 102), new Point2(70, 32), new Point2(0, 32) },
            plan.Footprint.Outer);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(3, 40.0, 8.0, 12.0, 30.0)]
    [InlineData(4, 40.0, 8.0, 12.0, 30.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 25.5, 6.0, 9.0, 18.0)]
    [InlineData(4, 12.0, 10.5, 7.25, 40.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int branches, double length, double mall, double shops, double anchor)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(new BranchingMallParameters(branches, length, mall, shops, anchor, 5.0, 0.0));
        double width = (2 * shops) + mall;

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal((width * width) + (branches * (length + anchor) * width), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(1 + (3 * branches), plan.Zones.Count);
        Assert.Equal((width * width) + (branches * length * mall), plan.Zones[0].FloorArea, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    public void EveryShopWingAndAnchorAdjoinsTheMall(int branches)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default with { BranchCount = branches });
        string[] retail = plan.Zones.Skip(1).Select(z => z.Id.Value).ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(retail.OrderBy(id => id, System.StringComparer.Ordinal), PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "MALL"));
        foreach (string zone in retail)
        {
            double shared = plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == zone && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "MALL").Sum(w => w.Length);
            Assert.Equal(zone.EndsWith("AN") ? 8.0 : 40.0 + 12.0, shared, 9);
            string branch = zone.Split('-')[0];
            string[] expected = zone.EndsWith("AN") ? new[] { $"{branch}-S1", $"{branch}-S2", "MALL" } : new[] { $"{branch}-AN", "MALL" };
            Assert.Equal(expected, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, zone));
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WithThreeBranchesTheMallsFreeSideIsItsSouthEntrance()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default);

        WallSurface entrance = Assert.Single(plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "MALL" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.South, Orientation.Bin(entrance.PlanAzimuth));
        Assert.Equal(32.0, entrance.Length, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "B1-AN"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "B1-S1"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.North }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "B1-S2"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WithFourBranchesTheMallHasNoOutdoorWall()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default with { BranchCount = 4 }, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Empty(PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(result.Value, "MALL"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "B4-S1", "B4-S2", "B4-AN" }, result.Value.Zones.Skip(10).Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(70, 30, 82, 70), Outer(result.Value, "B4-S1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(70, 0, 102, 30), Outer(result.Value, "B4-AN"));
        Assert.Equal((172.0 * 172.0) - (4 * 70.0 * 70.0), result.Value.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(16, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
        double retail = 3 * ((2 * 40.0) + 32.0 + (2 * 30.0)) * 5.0;
        Assert.Equal((0.2 * 32.0 * 5.0) + (0.3 * retail), result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, "BranchCount must be at least 3 (two branches are a bend, not a branching), got 2.")]
    [InlineData(5, "BranchCount must be at most 4 (branches leave the central court only on its four sides, D-100), got 5.")]
    public void BranchCountsOutsideThreeToFourAreErrors(int branches, string message)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default with { BranchCount = branches }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(message, error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 8.0, 12.0, 30.0, "BranchLength")]
    [InlineData(40.0, -8.0, 12.0, 30.0, "MallWidth")]
    [InlineData(40.0, 8.0, double.NaN, 30.0, "ShopDepth")]
    [InlineData(40.0, 8.0, 12.0, 0.0, "AnchorDepth")]
    [InlineData(40.0, double.PositiveInfinity, 12.0, 30.0, "MallWidth")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double length, double mall, double shops, double anchor, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new BranchingMallParameters(3, length, mall, shops, anchor, 5.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new BranchingMallParameters(1, 0.0, -8.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(7, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(new[] { ("Mall", SpaceType.Mall), ("Retail", SpaceType.Retail) }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.Mall, Examples.Retail }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(
            BranchingMallParameters.Default,
            new BranchingMallPresets(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Retail, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Mall));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Mall", "Retail" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(BranchingMallGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "AnchorDepth=30", "BranchCount=3", "BranchLength=40", "FloorHeight=5", "MallWidth=8", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=Mall:Example Mall;Retail:Example Retail", "ShopDepth=12",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(BranchingMallParameters.Default)), "branching-mall-canonical");
    }

    private static Point2[] Rectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY) => Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(BranchingMallParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/BranchingMall/BranchingMallGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.HubArms;

namespace Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;

/// <summary>Parameters of the branching public mall between retail wings (SYN-TYP-014, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="BranchCount">Number of mall branches from the central court, 3–4.</param>
/// <param name="BranchLength">Length of the shop wings along each branch, m.</param>
/// <param name="MallWidth">Width of the public mall in each branch, m.</param>
/// <param name="ShopDepth">Depth of the shop wing on each side of the mall, m.</param>
/// <param name="AnchorDepth">Depth of the anchor store at the end of each branch, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record BranchingMallParameters(
    int BranchCount,
    double BranchLength,
    double MallWidth,
    double ShopDepth,
    double AnchorDepth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 3 branches of 40 m shop wings, 12 m deep beside an 8 m mall, 30 m anchors, 5 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static BranchingMallParameters Default { get; } = new(3, 40.0, 8.0, 12.0, 30.0, 5.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the branching mall, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="Mall">Preset of the public mall; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Mall"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Retail">Preset of the shop wings and the anchors; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Retail"/>.</param>
public sealed record BranchingMallPresets(ProgramPreset Mall, ProgramPreset Retail) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Mall), SpaceType.Mall, Mall),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Retail), SpaceType.Retail, Retail),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Branching public mall between retail wings (SYN-TYP-014, ADR-006), on the shared <see cref="HubArmsLayout"/>: the hub is the mall's central court,
/// as wide as the branches (<c>2·ShopDepth + MallWidth</c>), and each branch holds, from the court outwards, a shop wing on either side of a mall strip
/// and then an anchor store across the branch's width. The mall, court and every strip, is one connected, branching zone reaching every branch (R002),
/// and every shop wing and anchor adjoins it (R001). Zones are departments (D-097, ADR-014): the shops of one side of a branch are one zone.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Zone IDs: <c>MALL</c>, then per branch <c>B{i}-S1</c>, <c>B{i}-S2</c> (the shop wings clockwise and counter-clockwise of the mall strip) and
/// <c>B{i}-AN</c> (the anchor), branch <c>i</c> from 1 in the order east, north, west, south. With three branches the court's south side is the mall's
/// entrance façade; with four the mall has no outdoor wall.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class BranchingMallGenerator : PlanGenerator<BranchingMallParameters, BranchingMallPresets>
{
    /// <summary>The fewest branches: two would be a bend, not a branching.</summary>
    public const int MinimumBranches = 3;

    /// <summary>The most branches: one per side of the court (D-100).</summary>
    public const int MaximumBranches = HubArmsLayout.MaximumArms;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public BranchingMallGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(BranchingMallGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(BranchingMallParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = HubArmsLayout.CountErrors(
            nameof(BranchingMallParameters.BranchCount),
            parameters.BranchCount,
            MinimumBranches,
            MaximumBranches,
            "two branches are a bend, not a branching",
            "branches leave the central court only on its four sides").ToList();
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(BranchingMallParameters.BranchLength), parameters.BranchLength),
            (nameof(BranchingMallParameters.MallWidth), parameters.MallWidth),
            (nameof(BranchingMallParameters.ShopDepth), parameters.ShopDepth),
            (nameof(BranchingMallParameters.AnchorDepth), parameters.AnchorDepth),
        };
        errors.AddRange(values.Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value)).Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}.")));
        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(BranchingMallParameters p)
    {
        double width = (2.0 * p.ShopDepth) + p.MallWidth;
        double mallEnd = p.ShopDepth + p.MallWidth;
        var layout = new HubArmsLayout(width, width, Enumerable.Repeat(p.BranchLength + p.AnchorDepth, p.BranchCount).ToArray());
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone> { new(new ZoneId("MALL"), "Mall", SpaceType.Mall, layout.HubWithStrips(p.ShopDepth, mallEnd, p.BranchLength)) };
        foreach (HubArm branch in layout.Arms)
        {
            string id = $"B{branch.Number}-";
            string name = $"Branch {branch.Number} ";
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId(id + "S1"), name + "Shops 1", SpaceType.Retail, branch.Piece(0.0, p.BranchLength, 0.0, p.ShopDepth)));
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId(id + "S2"), name + "Shops 2", SpaceType.Retail, branch.Piece(0.0, p.BranchLength, mallEnd, width)));
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId(id + "AN"), name + "Anchor", SpaceType.Retail, branch.Piece(p.BranchLength, branch.Length, 0.0, width)));
        }

        return new PlanLayout(layout.Footprint, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(BranchingMallParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BranchCount), p.BranchCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BranchLength), Provenance.Format(p.BranchLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.MallWidth), Provenance.Format(p.MallWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.ShopDepth), Provenance.Format(p.ShopDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.AnchorDepth), Provenance.Format(p.AnchorDepth)),
    };
}
```

82 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostBranches` (32, 3 and 4 branches), and `SemanticMergeKeepsTheMallWholeAndUnitesTheRetailOfEachBranch` (3 and 4 branches: the mall without a hole and one `Retail` zone of 1920 m² per branch). ```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.5 branching mall (SYN-TYP-014, ADR-006): one branching mall zone through a central court and three or four branches of shop wings and
/// anchors on the shared hub-and-arms layout, through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most branches.
/// </summary>
public sealed class BranchingMallTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest and the most branches (ADR-006, D-100).</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { BranchingMallGenerator.MinimumBranches, BranchingMallGenerator.MaximumBranches };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostBranches(string simplifier, string aggregator, int branches)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(BranchingMallParameters.Default with { BranchCount = branches }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsTheMallWholeAndUnitesTheRetailOfEachBranch(int branches)
    {
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan(BranchingMallParameters.Default with { BranchCount = branches })).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Mall }.Concat(Enumerable.Repeat(SpaceType.Retail, branches)), floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "MALL" }, floor.Zones[0].SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value));
        Assert.Empty(floor.Zones[0].Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        for (int branch = 1; branch <= branches; branch++)
        {
            Zone retail = floor.Zones[branch];
            Assert.Equal(new[] { $"B{branch}-S1", $"B{branch}-S2", $"B{branch}-AN" }, retail.SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value));
            Assert.Equal((2 * 480.0) + 960.0, retail.FloorArea, 6);
        }
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static BranchingMallPresets Presets() => new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Mall, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Retail);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(BranchingMallParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new BranchingMallGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? BranchingMallParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

## Slice B — `fix/perimeter-core-footprint-vertices`

### Task 4: *Perimeter Core* keeps the footprint's own vertices

The care hub of Task 5 has, by default, 14.4 m wings on a 16 m hub, which leave 0.8 m of hub façade beside each wing. On rotated plans, *Perimeter Core* followed by *Single Zone per Floor Type* or *Single Zone Building* failed with `FacadeNotCovered` ("Rebuilt façades cover 0 m of this 0.8 m wall"): `PerimeterCore` united each orientation bin's trapezoids with a plain `PolygonOps.Union`, which rounds every vertex to the clipping grid; the single-zone aggregators unite the perimeter zones as tiles and keep those rounded vertices, while the source walls lie on the exact footprint vertices, and for a 0.8 m wall the endpoint rounding (at most 0.7 grid units) turns its direction by up to about 2·10⁻⁶ rad, beyond `ToleranceSettings.Angle`. No part of the plan is too narrow, so this is a pipeline defect, not a precondition (D-087). This task fixes it test-first on a plan of its own, before the care hub exists: after the plain union, each vertex in the grid cell of a footprint vertex is replaced by that footprint vertex (`VertexGrid.Restore`, D-088); the mitred offset vertices keep their rounding, so the canonical linear plan's 45° corner walls, and every existing snapshot, stay as they are.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/Lod.Core/Simplification/Strategies.cs` (S3 file, last changed by `5038512` after `v0.8.2`), `docs/decisions/ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md` (last changed by `5038512`), `docs/decisions/ADR-002-geometry.md` (last changed in S8.4)
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/PerimeterCoreShortEdgeTests.cs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (S2–S8.4): `PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings, double)` and its bisector trapezoids (ADR-008), `PolygonOps.Union`, `VertexGrid(double)` with `Register` and `Restore` (D-088), `Polygon2.Create(outer, holes)`, `ToleranceSettings.Distance`; test support `PlanGenerator`, `PlanTransform.Apply`, `Pipeline.Aggregator`, `Pipeline.Tolerances`, `FloorEntry`, `BuildingValidator`, `ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit`.
- Produces: `PerimeterCore` registers every vertex of every footprint ring in a `VertexGrid` and maps each bin's union through the private `KeepFootprintVertices(Polygon2 union, VertexGrid footprintVertices)`, which restores every outer and hole vertex and falls back to the union if the restored polygon is not valid; no public API changes. `PerimeterCoreShortEdgeTests` (150 cases): a 20 m square block with an 18.4 m wing centred on its east side (0.8 m of the block's east façade either side of the wing, as at the care hub's corners), at 50 placements (rotation `7.2° · i + 0.7° · (i mod 3)`, translation `(3.7 i − 50, 20 − 2.3 i)` m): `PerimeterZonesKeepTheFootprintsOwnVertices` (50) and `TheSingleZoneMethodsCoverTheShortFacadesOfARotatedPerimeterCoreFloor` (100, *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* with 1, 3, 1). ADR-008 (point 6, a consequence, the status line) and ADR-002 (the unions-of-zones paragraph, the status line) record the rule.

git switch -c fix/perimeter-core-footprint-vertices main
```

- [x] **Step 2: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/PerimeterCoreShortEdgeTests.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// A footprint with façade edges shorter than 1 m for <see cref="PerimeterCore"/>: a 20 m square block with an 18.4 m wide wing centred on its east
/// side, which leaves 0.8 m of the block's east façade beside the wing, as at the hub corners of the care hub (SYN-TYP-018). A perimeter zone whose
/// vertices were rounded to the clipping grid gives such a short wall a direction up to about 2·10⁻⁶ rad off the source wall's on a rotated plan,
/// beyond the angular tolerance, so the single-zone methods found the rebuilt façade not to cover it (ADR-008).
/// </summary>
public sealed class PerimeterCoreShortEdgeTests
{
    private const double PerimeterDepth = 4.57;

    /// <summary>Rotations every 7.2° from 0° to 352.8°, each with its own translation, m.</summary>
    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Placements() =>
        Enumerable.Range(0, 50).Select(i => new object[] { (i * 7.2) + (0.7 * (i % 3)), (3.7 * i) - 50.0, 20.0 - (2.3 * i) });

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedAggregators() =>
        from placement in Placements()
        from aggregator in new[] { "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" }
        select placement.Append(aggregator).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Placements))]
    public void PerimeterZonesKeepTheFootprintsOwnVertices(double rotation, double dx, double dy)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(SteppedPlan(), rotation, dx, dy).Value;

        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(plan).Value;

        HashSet<Point2> vertices = floor.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value != "CORE").SelectMany(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint.Rings).SelectMany(r => r).ToHashSet();
        Assert.All(plan.Footprint.Outer, p => Assert.Contains(p, vertices));
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedAggregators))]
    public void TheSingleZoneMethodsCoverTheShortFacadesOfARotatedPerimeterCoreFloor(double rotation, double dx, double dy, string aggregator)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = PlanTransform.Apply(SteppedPlan(), rotation, dx, dy).Value;
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, PerimeterDepth).Simplify(plan).Value;

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(new[] { new FloorEntry(floor, 1), new FloorEntry(floor, 3), new FloorEntry(floor, 1) });

        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);
        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
    }

    private static IGeneratedPlan SteppedPlan() =>
        new SteppedGenerator().Generate(new SteppedParameters(), new SteppedPresets(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit)).Value;

    private sealed record SteppedParameters() : PlanParameters(3.0, 0.0);

    private sealed record SteppedPresets(ProgramPreset DwellingUnit) : PlanPresets
    {
        public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[] { new PresetSlot(nameof(DwellingUnit), SpaceType.DwellingUnit, DwellingUnit) };
    }

    private sealed class SteppedGenerator : PlanGenerator<SteppedParameters, SteppedPresets>
    {
        public SteppedGenerator()
            : base(ToleranceSettings.Default)
        {
        }

        public override string Name => nameof(SteppedGenerator);

        protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(SteppedParameters parameters) => Enumerable.Empty<Diagnostic>();

        protected override PlanLayout Layout(SteppedParameters p)
        {
            Polygon2 footprint = Polygon2.Create(new[]
            {
                new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(20, 0), new Point2(20, 0.8), new Point2(50, 0.8),
                new Point2(50, 19.2), new Point2(20, 19.2), new Point2(20, 20), new Point2(0, 20),
            }).Value;
            return new PlanLayout(footprint, new[]
            {
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("BLOCK"), "Block", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(0, 0, 20, 20)),
                new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("WING"), "Wing", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, Polygon2.Rectangle(20, 0.8, 50, 19.2)),
            });
        }

        protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(SteppedParameters p) => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — replace `src/Lod.Core/Simplification/Strategies.cs` (full file)**

Only `PerimeterCore` changes: one sentence of its summary, the `VertexGrid` of the footprint vertices, the union of each bin mapped through `KeepFootprintVertices`, and that private method. `SemanticMerge`, `NoSimplification`, `SingleZonePerFloor`, and every check of `PerimeterCore`, including the self-intersecting offset rings of `5038512`, are unchanged.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;

namespace Lod.Core.Simplification;

/// <summary>
/// Z1: merges zones of the same space type that are connected through shared walls. Each connected group becomes one zone, the union of its
/// footprints as tiles (<see cref="PolygonOps.UnionTiles"/>), so zones meeting at T-junctions unite exactly on rotated plans; the union may have
/// holes, where the group surrounds zones of another type.
/// </summary>
public sealed class SemanticMerge : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SemanticMerge(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SemanticMerge);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        Dictionary<ZoneId, Zone> byId = plan.Zones.ToDictionary(z => z.Id);
        ILookup<ZoneId, ZoneId> neighbours = plan.Surfaces
            .Where(s => s.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Interzone)
            .ToLookup(s => s.Zone, s => s.AdjacentZone!.Value);
        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        var visited = new HashSet<ZoneId>();
        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        var counters = new Dictionary<SpaceType, int>();
        foreach (Zone seed in plan.Zones.Where(z => !visited.Contains(z.Id)))
        {
            var group = new List<Zone>();
            var queue = new Queue<Zone>(new[] { seed });
            visited.Add(seed.Id);
            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                Zone current = queue.Dequeue();
                group.Add(current);
                foreach (ZoneId next in neighbours[current.Id].Where(n => !visited.Contains(n) && byId[n].SpaceType == seed.SpaceType))
                {
                    visited.Add(next);
                    queue.Enqueue(byId[next]);
                }
            }

            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> union = ops.UnionTiles(group.Select(z => z.Parts[0].Footprint).ToArray());
            if (union.Count != 1)
            {
                return Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(
                    DiagnosticCodes.DisconnectedGroup,
                    $"Merged group {string.Join(",", group.Select(z => z.Id.Value))} is not one polygon.",
                    seed.Id.Value));
            }

            int number = counters.TryGetValue(seed.SpaceType, out int n) ? n + 1 : 1;
            counters[seed.SpaceType] = number;
            targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"{seed.SpaceType}-{number}"), $"{seed.SpaceType} {number}", seed.SpaceType, union[0]));
        }

        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}

/// <summary>
/// Z2: perimeter zones of a configurable depth plus a core (ADR-008). Each footprint edge owns the trapezoid between the edge and its inward
/// mitred offset, so corners are split along the bisectors; trapezoids in the same orientation bin are merged, keeping the footprint's own vertices.
/// A footprint with holes (courts) is
/// offset on every ring: the edges of the holes face into their courts and give court perimeter zones <c>P-Court-&lt;Bin&gt;</c>, separate from
/// the outer façades' zones, and the core is what lies between the offset rings, a ring around each court.
/// </summary>
public sealed class PerimeterCore : PlanSimplifier
{
    private readonly double _depth;

    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    /// <param name="perimeterDepth">Perimeter zone depth, m.</param>
    public PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings tolerances, double perimeterDepth)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
        _depth = perimeterDepth;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(PerimeterCore);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        if (!(_depth > 0) || double.IsInfinity(_depth))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"Perimeter depth must be positive, got {_depth}.");
        }

        // Every ring keeps the floor on its left (outer ring counter-clockwise, holes clockwise), so one mitre rule offsets each ring into the floor.
        IReadOnlyList<Point2>[] rings = plan.Footprint.Rings.ToArray();
        Point2[][] offsets = rings.Select(Offset).ToArray();
        Result<Polygon2> core = Polygon2.Create(offsets[0], offsets.Skip(1));
        bool ringsValid = Enumerable.Range(0, rings.Length).All(r =>
            KeepsDirections(rings[r], offsets[r]) && (r == 0 ? Polygon2.SignedArea(offsets[r]) > 0 : Polygon2.SignedArea(offsets[r]) < 0) && Polygon2.Create(offsets[r]).IsSuccess && IsSimple(offsets[r]));
        if (!core.IsSuccess || !ringsValid)
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter depth {_depth} m leaves no valid core for this footprint: {Precondition()}");
        }

        var ops = new PolygonOps(Tolerances);
        if (rings.Length > 1)
        {
            // The offset holes must lie inside the offset outer ring and apart from each other; otherwise a wing between the outer and a court façade,
            // or between two courts, is not wider than twice the depth and the perimeter bands overlap.
            IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> between = ops.Difference(Polygon2.Create(offsets[0]).Value, offsets.Skip(1).Select(h => Polygon2.Create(h).Value));
            if (between.Count != 1 || !Tolerances.AreaEquals(core.Value.Area, between.Sum(p => p.Area)))
            {
                return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter depth {_depth} m leaves no valid core between the outer and the court façades: {Precondition()}");
            }
        }

        var footprintVertices = new VertexGrid(Tolerances.Distance);
        foreach (Point2 vertex in rings.SelectMany(r => r))
        {
            footprintVertices.Register(vertex);
        }

        var trapezoids = new List<(bool Court, OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)>();
        for (int r = 0; r < rings.Length; r++)
        {
            IReadOnlyList<Point2> v = rings[r];
            Point2[] offset = offsets[r];
            int n = v.Count;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
                Polygon2 shape = Polygon2.Create(new[] { v[i], v[(i + 1) % n], offset[(i + 1) % n], offset[i] }).Value;
                trapezoids.Add((r > 0, Orientation.Bin(Orientation.OutwardAzimuth(v[i], v[(i + 1) % n]) + plan.OrientationDegrees), shape));
            }
        }

        double tiled = trapezoids.Sum(t => t.Shape.Area) + core.Value.Area;
        if (!Tolerances.AreaEquals(plan.Footprint.Area, tiled))
        {
            return Fail(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, $"Perimeter zones and core cover {tiled:0.###} m² of a {plan.Footprint.Area:0.###} m² footprint: {Precondition()}");
        }

        var targets = new List<TargetZone>();
        foreach (bool court in new[] { false, true })
        {
            string id = court ? "P-Court-" : "P-";
            string name = court ? "Perimeter Court " : "Perimeter ";
            foreach (IGrouping<OrientationBin, (bool Court, OrientationBin Bin, Polygon2 Shape)> bin in trapezoids.Where(t => t.Court == court).GroupBy(t => t.Bin).OrderBy(g => g.Key))
            {
                IReadOnlyList<Polygon2> merged = ops.Union(bin.Select(t => t.Shape)).Select(u => KeepFootprintVertices(u, footprintVertices)).ToArray();
                for (int k = 0; k < merged.Count; k++)
                {
                    string suffix = merged.Count == 1 ? string.Empty : $"-{k + 1}";
                    targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId($"{id}{bin.Key}{suffix}"), $"{name}{bin.Key}{suffix}", SpaceType.Mixed, merged[k]));
                }
            }
        }

        targets.Add(new TargetZone(new ZoneId("CORE"), "Core", SpaceType.Mixed, core.Value));
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(targets);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() =>
        new[] { new KeyValuePair<string, string>("PerimeterDepth", Provenance.Format(_depth)) };

    /// <summary>The precondition a <see cref="DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow"/> plan breaks, with what the user can do (ADR-008).</summary>
    private string Precondition() =>
        $"every wing and every part of the plan must be wider than twice the depth ({2.0 * _depth} m). Use a smaller depth or another simplifier.";

    /// <summary>The mitred inward offset of every vertex of a ring, so offset vertex <c>i</c> belongs to ring vertex <c>i</c> (ADR-008).</summary>
    private Point2[] Offset(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring)
    {
        int n = ring.Count;
        return Enumerable.Range(0, n).Select(i => Mitre(ring[(i + n - 1) % n], ring[i], ring[(i + 1) % n])).ToArray();
    }

    /// <summary>Whether every offset edge keeps the direction of its ring edge; a reversed edge means the depth is too large for the edge.</summary>
    private static bool KeepsDirections(IReadOnlyList<Point2> ring, Point2[] offset)
    {
        int n = ring.Count;
        return Enumerable.Range(0, n).All(i =>
        {
            Point2 a = ring[i];
            Point2 b = ring[(i + 1) % n];
            Point2 c = offset[i];
            Point2 d = offset[(i + 1) % n];
            return ((b.X - a.X) * (d.X - c.X)) + ((b.Y - a.Y) * (d.Y - c.Y)) > 0;
        });
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Whether an offset ring is simple: no two non-adjacent edges come within the distance tolerance of each other, so the ring neither crosses
    /// nor touches itself. A part of the footprint not wider than twice the depth that no single edge spans, such as a neck between two blocks,
    /// keeps every offset edge's direction and the signed areas still add up, but its two offset façades cross (ADR-008).
    /// </summary>
    private bool IsSimple(Point2[] ring)
    {
        int n = ring.Length;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            for (int j = i + 2; j < (i == 0 ? n - 1 : n); j++)
            {
                if (SegmentDistance(ring[i], ring[i + 1], ring[j], ring[(j + 1) % n]) <= Tolerances.Distance)
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>The shortest distance between segments <c>ab</c> and <c>cd</c>; zero when they cross.</summary>
    private static double SegmentDistance(Point2 a, Point2 b, Point2 c, Point2 d)
    {
        if (Cross(a, b, c) * Cross(a, b, d) < 0 && Cross(c, d, a) * Cross(c, d, b) < 0)
        {
            return 0.0;
        }

        return Math.Min(Math.Min(PointSegmentDistance(a, c, d), PointSegmentDistance(b, c, d)), Math.Min(PointSegmentDistance(c, a, b), PointSegmentDistance(d, a, b)));
    }

    /// <summary>The z component of <c>(b − a) × (p − a)</c>: positive when <paramref name="p"/> lies left of <c>ab</c>.</summary>
    private static double Cross(Point2 a, Point2 b, Point2 p) => ((b.X - a.X) * (p.Y - a.Y)) - ((b.Y - a.Y) * (p.X - a.X));

    private static double PointSegmentDistance(Point2 p, Point2 a, Point2 b)
    {
        double dx = b.X - a.X;
        double dy = b.Y - a.Y;
        double t = Math.Max(0.0, Math.Min(1.0, (((p.X - a.X) * dx) + ((p.Y - a.Y) * dy)) / ((dx * dx) + (dy * dy))));
        return p.DistanceTo(new Point2(a.X + (t * dx), a.Y + (t * dy)));
    }

    private Point2 Mitre(Point2 previous, Point2 vertex, Point2 next)
    {
        (double X, double Y) a = InwardNormal(previous, vertex);
        (double X, double Y) b = InwardNormal(vertex, next);
        double scale = _depth / (1.0 + (a.X * b.X) + (a.Y * b.Y));
        return new Point2(vertex.X + ((a.X + b.X) * scale), vertex.Y + ((a.Y + b.Y) * scale));
    }

    private static (double X, double Y) InwardNormal(Point2 from, Point2 to)
    {
        double length = from.DistanceTo(to);
        return (-(to.Y - from.Y) / length, (to.X - from.X) / length);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The union of a bin's trapezoids with every vertex in the grid cell of a footprint vertex replaced by that vertex. Clipping rounds every vertex
    /// to the grid, up to 0.7 grid units off; on a rotated plan that turns a façade edge shorter than about 1 m by more than the angular tolerance,
    /// so a single-zone aggregator would find its outline not to cover the source wall (ADR-008). The offset vertices keep their rounding.
    /// </summary>
    private static Polygon2 KeepFootprintVertices(Polygon2 union, VertexGrid footprintVertices) =>
        Polygon2.Create(union.Outer.Select(footprintVertices.Restore), union.Holes.Select(h => h.Select(footprintVertices.Restore))) is { IsSuccess: true } restored
            ? restored.Value
            : union;

    private static Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> Fail(string code, string message) =>
        Result.Failure<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(Diagnostic.Error(code, message));
}

/// <summary>Z3: the whole floor as one zone.</summary>
public sealed class SingleZonePerFloor : PlanSimplifier
{
    /// <summary>Creates the simplifier.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public SingleZonePerFloor(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(SingleZonePerFloor);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override Result<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>> ProposeZones(IGeneratedPlan plan)
    {
        SpaceType[] types = plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType).Distinct().ToArray();
        SpaceType type = types.Length == 1 ? types[0] : SpaceType.Mixed;
        return Result.Success<IReadOnlyList<TargetZone>>(new[] { new TargetZone(new ZoneId("FLOOR"), "Floor", type, plan.Footprint) });
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe() => Enumerable.Empty<KeyValuePair<string, string>>();
}
```

```markdown
- Footprints with holes (S8.3, the enclosed court `HDHM-TYP-005` of [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)): every ring gets bisector trapezoids, so a court façade is zoned like an outer façade, and the core is a ring around the court. The court façades get their own zones because they are different façades from the outer façades of the same bin, facing a court instead of the street; uniting them with the outer zone of their bin would always give two separate polygons (the core lies between them), numbered `P-North-1`, `P-North-2` in an order that says nothing about which is which. For the default enclosed court (48 m × 48 m with a 24 m × 24 m court, 12 m wings) with `d` = 4.57 m: `P-North`, `P-East`, `P-South`, `P-West` 198.4751 m² each, `P-Court-North` … `P-Court-West` 130.5649 m² each, and a `CORE` ring of 38.86² − 33.14² = 411.84 m². Every wing between the outer and a court façade must be wider than `2d` (D-087): 9 m wings fail with `PlanTooNarrow`. The bands of the outer and court façades never touch, so no trapezoid of a hole is ever united with one of the outer ring.

On a rotated plan that turns a footprint edge shorter than about 1 m, such as the 0.8 m of hub façade beside a 14.4 m wing on a 16 m hub, by more than the angular tolerance (ADR-004), so *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* found that the outline united from the perimeter zones did not cover the short source wall (`FacadeNotCovered`) at about one rotation in five. Restoring the footprint's vertices after the union puts the rebuilt outline exactly on the source walls. Only footprint vertices are restored: restoring the offset vertices too (a union as tiles) would move the 45° corner walls of the canonical linear plan by less than the grid and flip their facing bin at the North/East boundary, changing results for no gain. No tolerance changed, and every existing result is unchanged.

2. Replace

```markdown
**Unions of zones (S8.2).** Wherever zones are united into one zone, their footprints are united as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)): the groups of *Semantic Merge* (Z1) as well as the storey outlines of the single-zone floor aggregators. A plain union of footprints that meet at T-junctions, such as the pinwheel dwellings of the point plate (`HDHM-TYP-007`, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)), splits the group or leaves slits once the plan is rotated off the axes, because rounding on the clipping grid moves the T-junction off its edge. A united zone may have holes, where it surrounds zones of another type (the point plate's dwellings around the core). The union keeps the tiles' own vertices: clipping rounds every vertex to the grid, up to 0.7 grid units off its true position, so each vertex of the result is replaced by the tile vertex of its grid cell. United zones therefore lie on their sources' façade lines, and uniting zones that were themselves united, as the single-zone aggregators do with a *Semantic Merge* floor, adds no rounding; without this, a rotated *Semantic Merge* floor could fail *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* with `FacadeNotCovered` (S8.2).
```

```markdown
**Unions of zones (S8.2).** Wherever zones are united into one zone, their footprints are united as tiles (`PolygonOps.UnionTiles`, [ADR-013](ADR-013-vertical-aggregation-methods.md)): the groups of *Semantic Merge* (Z1) as well as the storey outlines of the single-zone floor aggregators. A plain union of footprints that meet at T-junctions, such as the pinwheel dwellings of the point plate (`HDHM-TYP-007`, [ADR-006](ADR-006-plan-generation-mechanism.md)), splits the group or leaves slits once the plan is rotated off the axes, because rounding on the clipping grid moves the T-junction off its edge. A united zone may have holes, where it surrounds zones of another type (the point plate's dwellings around the core). The union keeps the tiles' own vertices: clipping rounds every vertex to the grid, up to 0.7 grid units off its true position, so each vertex of the result is replaced by the tile vertex of its grid cell. United zones therefore lie on their sources' façade lines, and uniting zones that were themselves united, as the single-zone aggregators do with a *Semantic Merge* floor, adds no rounding; without this, a rotated *Semantic Merge* floor could fail *Single Zone per Floor Type* and *Single Zone Building* with `FacadeNotCovered` (S8.2). *Perimeter Core* (Z2) unites the trapezoids of one orientation bin with a plain union, because they meet edge to edge on the bisectors, and then replaces every vertex in the grid cell of a footprint vertex by that vertex, so its zones lie on the footprint's own vertices too; without this, a footprint edge shorter than about 1 m, such as a hub corner beside a narrower arm, could fail the same check on a rotated plan (S8.5, [ADR-008](ADR-008-perimeter-core-corners.md)).
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.CareHub;

public sealed class CareHubGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly CareHubPresets Examples = new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareBedroom, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareCommunal, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    private static readonly string[] CanonicalZones =
    {
        "HUB", "W1-CO", "W1-B1", "W1-B2", "W2-CO", "W2-B1", "W2-B2", "W3-CO", "W3-B1", "W3-B2",
    };

    private readonly CareHubGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new CareHubParameters(WingCount: 3, WingLength: 30.0, BedroomDepth: 6.0, CorridorWidth: 2.4, HubWidth: 16.0, FloorHeight: 3.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            CareHubParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsAHubWithThreeWingsOfBedroomsOffACorridor()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CareHubParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(CanonicalZones, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "Communal Hub", "Wing 1 Corridor", "Wing 1 Bedrooms 1", "Wing 1 Bedrooms 2", "Wing 2 Corridor", "Wing 2 Bedrooms 1", "Wing 2 Bedrooms 2",
                "Wing 3 Corridor", "Wing 3 Bedrooms 1", "Wing 3 Bedrooms 2",
            },
            plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(SpaceType.CareCommunal, plan.Zones[0].SpaceType);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.EndsWith("CO")), z => Assert.Equal(SpaceType.Corridor, z.SpaceType));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.Contains("-B")), z => Assert.Equal(SpaceType.CareBedroom, z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(256.0 + (3 * 30.0 * 14.4), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(256.0, plan.Zones[0].FloorArea, 9);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.EndsWith("CO")), z => Assert.Equal(72.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones.Where(z => z.Id.Value.Contains("-B")), z => Assert.Equal(180.0, z.FloorArea, 9));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 0, 46, 16), Outer(plan, "HUB"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(46, 6.8, 76, 9.2), Outer(plan, "W1-CO"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(46, 0.8, 76, 6.8), Outer(plan, "W1-B1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(46, 9.2, 76, 15.2), Outer(plan, "W1-B2"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(36.8, 16, 39.2, 46), Outer(plan, "W2-CO"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(39.2, 16, 45.2, 46), Outer(plan, "W2-B1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(0, 9.2, 30, 15.2), Outer(plan, "W3-B1"));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(3.0, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, 30.0, 6.0, 2.4, 16.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 30.0, 6.0, 2.4, 16.0)]
    [InlineData(4, 30.0, 6.0, 2.4, 16.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 22.5, 5.5, 2.0, 13.0)]
    [InlineData(4, 41.0, 4.8, 1.8, 11.4)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int wings, double length, double bedrooms, double corridor, double hub)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(new CareHubParameters(wings, length, bedrooms, corridor, hub, 3.0, 0.0));

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal((hub * hub) + (wings * length * ((2 * bedrooms) + corridor)), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(1 + (3 * wings), plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    [InlineData(4)]
    public void EveryWingCorridorAdjoinsTheHub(int wings)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CareHubParameters.Default with { WingCount = wings });

        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Skip(1).Select(z => z.Id.Value).OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal), PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "HUB"));
        for (int wing = 1; wing <= wings; wing++)
        {
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "HUB", $"W{wing}-B1", $"W{wing}-B2" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, $"W{wing}-CO"));
            Assert.Equal(2.4, SharedLength(plan, $"W{wing}-CO", "HUB"), 9);
            foreach (string bedrooms in new[] { $"W{wing}-B1", $"W{wing}-B2" })
            {
                Assert.Equal(new[] { "HUB", $"W{wing}-CO" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, bedrooms));
                Assert.Equal(30.0, SharedLength(plan, bedrooms, $"W{wing}-CO"), 9);
                Assert.Equal(6.0, SharedLength(plan, bedrooms, "HUB"), 9);
            }
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TwoWingsLeaveTheHubEastAndNorth()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CareHubParameters.Default with { WingCount = 2 });

        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(0, 0, 16, 16), Outer(plan, "HUB"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(16, 6.8, 46, 9.2), Outer(plan, "W1-CO"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(6.8, 16, 9.2, 46), Outer(plan, "W2-CO"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "HUB"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void TheHubCornersBesideTheWingsAreTooShortForAWindow()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CareHubParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess);
        WallSurface[] corners = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>()
            .Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "HUB" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Length < 1.0)
            .ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(6, corners.Length);
        Assert.All(corners, w => Assert.Equal(0.8, w.Length, 9));
        Assert.All(corners, w => Assert.Empty(w.Windows));
        Assert.Equal(corners.Select(w => w.Id.Value), result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted).Select(d => d.Subject));
        Assert.All(result.Diagnostics, d => Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.WindowOmitted, d.Code));
        WallSurface south = Assert.Single(result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "HUB" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors && w.Length > 1.0);
        Assert.Equal(16.0, south.Length, 9);
        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.South, Orientation.Bin(south.PlanAzimuth));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallAboveTheLimitsHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CareHubParameters.Default with { HubWidth = 14.4 }, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(1 + (3 * 5), result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
        double bedrooms = 3 * 2 * (30.0 + 6.0) * 3.0;
        double corridors = 3 * 2.4 * 3.0;
        Assert.Equal((0.4 * 14.4 * 3.0) + (0.3 * bedrooms) + (0.2 * corridors), result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryWingCorridorEndsInAnOutdoorWallWithAWindow()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(CareHubParameters.Default);

        for (int wing = 1; wing <= 3; wing++)
        {
            WallSurface end = Assert.Single(plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == $"W{wing}-CO" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
            Assert.Equal(2.4, end.Length, 9);
            Assert.Single(end.Windows);
        }
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, "WingCount must be at least 2 (the hub serves several bedroom groups), got 1.")]
    [InlineData(5, "WingCount must be at most 4 (wings leave the hub only on its four sides, D-100), got 5.")]
    public void WingCountsOutsideTwoToFourAreErrors(int wings, string message)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CareHubParameters.Default with { WingCount = wings }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(message, error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 6.0, 2.4, 16.0, "WingLength")]
    [InlineData(30.0, -6.0, 2.4, 16.0, "BedroomDepth")]
    [InlineData(30.0, 6.0, double.NaN, 16.0, "CorridorWidth")]
    [InlineData(30.0, 6.0, 2.4, 0.0, "HubWidth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 6.0, 2.4, 16.0, "WingLength")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double length, double bedrooms, double corridor, double hub, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new CareHubParameters(3, length, bedrooms, corridor, hub, 3.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWingWiderThanTheHubIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CareHubParameters.Default with { BedroomDepth = 8.0 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            "The wing width 2·BedroomDepth + CorridorWidth 18.4 m is wider than HubWidth 16 m; an arm must not be wider than the hub it leaves, "
            + "or it would overlap its neighbour beyond the hub's corner.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AWingAsWideAsTheHubIsValid()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(CareHubParameters.Default with { HubWidth = 14.4 }, Examples);

        Assert.True(result.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", result.Diagnostics));
        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(result.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new CareHubParameters(1, 0.0, -6.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(7, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("CareBedroom", SpaceType.CareBedroom), ("CareCommunal", SpaceType.CareCommunal), ("Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor) },
            Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.CareBedroom, Examples.CareCommunal, Examples.Corridor }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(
            CareHubParameters.Default,
            new CareHubPresets(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareCommunal, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareBedroom, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "CareBedroom", "CareCommunal" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(CareHubParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(CareHubParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(CareHubParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(CareHubGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "BedroomDepth=6", "CorridorWidth=2.4", "FloorHeight=3", "HubWidth=16", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=CareBedroom:Example Care Bedroom;CareCommunal:Example Care Communal;Corridor:Example Corridor", "WingCount=3", "WingLength=30",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(CareHubParameters.Default)), "care-hub-canonical");
    }

    private static double SharedLength(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone, string other) =>
        plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == zone && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == other).Sum(w => w.Length);

    private static Point2[] Rectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY) => Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();

    /// <summary>A zone's outer ring rounded to 9 decimals: the arms of the default plan are offset by 0.8 m, which is not exact in binary.</summary>
    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) =>
        plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.Select(p => new Point2(Math.Round(p.X, 9), Math.Round(p.Y, 9))).ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(CareHubParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}

- [x] **Step 4: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/CareHub/CareHubGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.HubArms;

namespace Lod.Generators.CareHub;

/// <summary>Parameters of the communal hub serving elder-care bedroom groups (SYN-TYP-018, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="WingCount">Number of bedroom wings around the hub, 2–4.</param>
/// <param name="WingLength">Length of each wing from the hub to its end, m.</param>
/// <param name="BedroomDepth">Depth of the bedrooms on each side of a wing corridor, m.</param>
/// <param name="CorridorWidth">Width of each wing corridor, m.</param>
/// <param name="HubWidth">Side of the square communal hub, m; at least the wing width <c>2·BedroomDepth + CorridorWidth</c>.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record CareHubParameters(
    int WingCount,
    double WingLength,
    double BedroomDepth,
    double CorridorWidth,
    double HubWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 3 wings of 30 m, 6 m bedrooms either side of a 2.4 m corridor, a 16 m hub, 3 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static CareHubParameters Default { get; } = new(3, 30.0, 6.0, 2.4, 16.0, 3.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the care hub, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="CareBedroom">Preset of the bedroom groups; space type <see cref="SpaceType.CareBedroom"/>.</param>
/// <param name="CareCommunal">Preset of the communal hub; space type <see cref="SpaceType.CareCommunal"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Corridor">Preset of the wing corridors; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Corridor"/>.</param>
public sealed record CareHubPresets(ProgramPreset CareBedroom, ProgramPreset CareCommunal, ProgramPreset Corridor) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(CareBedroom), SpaceType.CareBedroom, CareBedroom),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(CareCommunal), SpaceType.CareCommunal, CareCommunal),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Corridor), SpaceType.Corridor, Corridor),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Communal hub serving elder-care bedroom groups (SYN-TYP-018, ADR-006), on the shared <see cref="HubArmsLayout"/>: bedroom wings are arranged
/// around the communal and service hub (R001), and circulation branches from it (R002): each wing is a corridor along its axis from the hub to the
/// wing's end, with a group of bedrooms on each side, so every wing corridor adjoins the hub. Zones are departments (D-097, ADR-014): the bedrooms of
/// one side of a wing are one zone.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Zone IDs: <c>HUB</c>, then per wing <c>W{i}-CO</c> (the corridor), <c>W{i}-B1</c>, <c>W{i}-B2</c> (the bedrooms clockwise and counter-clockwise
/// of it), wing <c>i</c> from 1 in the order east, north, west, south; two wings leave the hub east and north. Wings narrower than the hub leave its
/// corners as short outdoor walls, which get no window below the ADR-011 limits (0.8 m by default).
/// </remarks>
public sealed class CareHubGenerator : PlanGenerator<CareHubParameters, CareHubPresets>
{
    /// <summary>The fewest wings: the hub serves several bedroom groups.</summary>
    public const int MinimumWings = 2;

    /// <summary>The most wings: one per side of the hub (D-100).</summary>
    public const int MaximumWings = HubArmsLayout.MaximumArms;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public CareHubGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(CareHubGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(CareHubParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = HubArmsLayout.CountErrors(
            nameof(CareHubParameters.WingCount),
            parameters.WingCount,
            MinimumWings,
            MaximumWings,
            "the hub serves several bedroom groups",
            "wings leave the hub only on its four sides").ToList();
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(CareHubParameters.WingLength), parameters.WingLength),
            (nameof(CareHubParameters.BedroomDepth), parameters.BedroomDepth),
            (nameof(CareHubParameters.CorridorWidth), parameters.CorridorWidth),
            (nameof(CareHubParameters.HubWidth), parameters.HubWidth),
        };
        var lengths = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        if (lengths.Count == 0)
        {
            lengths.AddRange(HubArmsLayout.WidthErrors(
                $"The wing width 2·{nameof(CareHubParameters.BedroomDepth)} + {nameof(CareHubParameters.CorridorWidth)}",
                WingWidth(parameters),
                nameof(CareHubParameters.HubWidth),
                parameters.HubWidth));
        }

        errors.AddRange(lengths);
        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(CareHubParameters p)
    {
        double width = WingWidth(p);
        double corridorEnd = p.BedroomDepth + p.CorridorWidth;
        var layout = new HubArmsLayout(p.HubWidth, width, Enumerable.Repeat(p.WingLength, p.WingCount).ToArray());
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone> { new(new ZoneId("HUB"), "Communal Hub", SpaceType.CareCommunal, layout.Hub) };
        foreach (HubArm wing in layout.Arms)
        {
            string id = $"W{wing.Number}-";
            string name = $"Wing {wing.Number} ";
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId(id + "CO"), name + "Corridor", SpaceType.Corridor, wing.Piece(0.0, wing.Length, p.BedroomDepth, corridorEnd)));
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId(id + "B1"), name + "Bedrooms 1", SpaceType.CareBedroom, wing.Piece(0.0, wing.Length, 0.0, p.BedroomDepth)));
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId(id + "B2"), name + "Bedrooms 2", SpaceType.CareBedroom, wing.Piece(0.0, wing.Length, corridorEnd, width)));
        }

        return new PlanLayout(layout.Footprint, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(CareHubParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingCount), p.WingCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.WingLength), Provenance.Format(p.WingLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.BedroomDepth), Provenance.Format(p.BedroomDepth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.CorridorWidth), Provenance.Format(p.CorridorWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.HubWidth), Provenance.Format(p.HubWidth)),
    };

    private static double WingWidth(CareHubParameters p) => (2.0 * p.BedroomDepth) + p.CorridorWidth;
}
```
