- [x] **Step 8: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/CareHubTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

82 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostWings` (32, 2 and 4 wings), `SemanticMergeKeepsEveryDepartmentBecauseNoTwoOfOneTypeTouch` (all ten zones, each from itself), and `PerimeterCoreGivesEachHubCornerItsOwnSmallPerimeterZone` (six zones of 0.8 · 4.57 m² from `HUB`). No production code changes. Without the change of Task 4, the cases of *Perimeter Core* with *Single Zone per Floor Type* and with *Single Zone Building* at the 131.4° placement fail with `FacadeNotCovered` (note 6).

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.5 care hub (SYN-TYP-018, ADR-006): two to four wings of bedrooms off a corridor around a communal hub on the shared hub-and-arms layout,
/// with 0.8 m hub corners beside the 14.4 m wings, through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most wings.
/// </summary>
public sealed class CareHubTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest and the most wings (ADR-006, D-100).</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { CareHubGenerator.MinimumWings, CareHubGenerator.MaximumWings };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostWings(string simplifier, string aggregator, int wings)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(CareHubParameters.Default with { WingCount = wings }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsEveryDepartmentBecauseNoTwoOfOneTypeTouch()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan();

        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType), floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value), floor.Zones.Select(z => Assert.Single(z.SourceZones).Value));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => System.Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 6)), floor.Zones.Select(z => System.Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 6)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreGivesEachHubCornerItsOwnSmallPerimeterZone()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Zone[] corners = floor.Zones.Where(z => z.SourceZones.Count == 1 && z.SourceZones[0].Value == "HUB" && z.Id.Value != "CORE" && z.FloorArea < 5.0).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(6, corners.Length);
        Assert.All(corners, z => Assert.Equal(0.8 * 4.57, z.FloorArea, 6));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static CareHubPresets Presets() => new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareBedroom, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareCommunal, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(CareHubParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new CareHubGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? CareHubParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

### Task 6: Public foyer branching to activity halls (`SYN-TYP-019`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Lod.Generators/FoyerHalls/FoyerHallsGenerator.cs`
- Test: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/FoyerHalls/FoyerHallsGeneratorTests.cs`, `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/FoyerHallsTypologyTests.cs`
- Snapshot: create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/FoyerHalls/Snapshots/foyer-halls-canonical.txt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: as Task 2, with `SpaceType.Lobby`, `ActivityHall`, and `Service`, and `ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Lobby`, `ActivityHall`, and `Service` (S8.4).
- Produces: `public sealed record FoyerHallsParameters(int HallCount, double HallLength, double HallWidth, double SupportLength, double FoyerWidth, double FloorHeight, double OrientationDegrees) : PlanParameters` with `static Default` = (2, 30, 20, 10, 20, 6, 0); `public sealed record FoyerHallsPresets(ProgramPreset Lobby, ProgramPreset ActivityHall, ProgramPreset Service) : PlanPresets` (slots `Lobby`, `ActivityHall`, `Service`); `public sealed class FoyerHallsGenerator(ToleranceSettings)`, `Name` = `FoyerHallsGenerator`, `public const int MinimumHalls = 2`, `MaximumHalls = 3`: the foyer `FO` (*Foyer*, `Lobby`) is the hub; the arms in the order east, north, west, south are hall 1, the support wing `SV` (*Support*, `Service`, `SupportLength` long), hall 2, and hall 3 (`H{i}`, *Hall i*, `ActivityHall`, `HallLength` long), all `HallWidth` wide; zones are listed `FO`, `H1…Hn`, `SV` (ADR-006's section of the family; D-102); errors `InvalidParameter` for a hall count outside 2–3 (`HallCount must be at least 2 (the foyer serves several distinct activity spaces), got 1.`, `HallCount must be at most 3 (the support wing takes one of the foyer's four sides, D-100), got 4.`), for each length that is not positive and finite, and, only when those pass, for halls wider than the foyer; provenance keys `HallCount`, `HallLength`, `HallWidth`, `SupportLength`, `FoyerWidth`. By default a T of 1800 m²: the foyer 400 m², two halls of 600 m², the support wing 200 m²; 10 windows with 276 m² of glazing, the foyer's one window on its south entrance façade; with three halls as wide as the foyer the foyer has no outdoor wall.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests — create `tests/Lod.Generators.Tests/FoyerHalls/FoyerHallsGeneratorTests.cs` (full file)**

27 tests: the ADR-006 defaults; the canonical plan (zone IDs, names, space types, areas); a third hall south of the foyer and listed before the support wing; tiling for four sizes and counts; every hall and the support wing adjoining the foyer (2 and 3 halls); the foyer's south entrance façade with two halls and no outdoor wall with three; halls narrower than the foyer leaving its corners outdoor; the window rule; two hall counts outside 2–3; five invalid lengths; a hall wider than the foyer; every invalid parameter reported at once; preset slots; swapped presets; determinism; provenance; the canonical snapshot.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Geometry;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Lod.Tests.Shared;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Generators.Tests.FoyerHalls;

public sealed class FoyerHallsGeneratorTests
{
    /// <summary>The example presets, one per slot.</summary>
    public static readonly FoyerHallsPresets Examples = new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Lobby, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ActivityHall, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Service);

    private readonly FoyerHallsGenerator _generator = new(ToleranceSettings.Default);

    [Fact]
    public void DefaultsAreTheAdr006Defaults()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new FoyerHallsParameters(HallCount: 2, HallLength: 30.0, HallWidth: 20.0, SupportLength: 10.0, FoyerWidth: 20.0, FloorHeight: 6.0, OrientationDegrees: 0.0),
            FoyerHallsParameters.Default);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanIsAFoyerWithTwoHallsAndASupportWing()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default);

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "FO", "H1", "H2", "SV" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Foyer", "Hall 1", "Hall 2", "Support" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Name));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Lobby, SpaceType.ActivityHall, SpaceType.ActivityHall, SpaceType.Service }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(1800.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { 400.0, 600.0, 600.0, 200.0 }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.FloorArea));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 0, 50, 20), Outer(plan, "FO"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(50, 0, 80, 20), Outer(plan, "H1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(0, 0, 30, 20), Outer(plan, "H2"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 20, 50, 30), Outer(plan, "SV"));
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { new Point2(0, 0), new Point2(80, 0), new Point2(80, 20), new Point2(50, 20), new Point2(50, 30), new Point2(30, 30), new Point2(30, 20), new Point2(0, 20) },
            plan.Footprint.Outer);
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Equal(6.0, z.Parts[0].Height));
        Assert.All(plan.Zones, z => Assert.Empty(z.SourceZones));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AThirdHallLeavesTheFoyerSouthAndIsListedBeforeTheSupportWing()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = 3 });

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "FO", "H1", "H2", "H3", "SV" }, plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 30, 50, 50), Outer(plan, "FO"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(50, 30, 80, 50), Outer(plan, "H1"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(0, 30, 30, 50), Outer(plan, "H2"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 0, 50, 30), Outer(plan, "H3"));
        Assert.Equal(Rectangle(30, 50, 50, 60), Outer(plan, "SV"));
        Assert.Equal(2400.0, plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2, 30.0, 20.0, 10.0, 20.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 30.0, 20.0, 10.0, 20.0)]
    [InlineData(2, 24.0, 15.0, 8.0, 18.0)]
    [InlineData(3, 40.0, 12.5, 6.0, 16.0)]
    public void ZonesTileTheFootprint(int halls, double length, double width, double support, double foyer)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(new FoyerHallsParameters(halls, length, width, support, foyer, 6.0, 0.0));

        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(plan);
        Assert.Equal((foyer * foyer) + (halls * length * width) + (support * width), plan.Footprint.Area, 9);
        Assert.Equal(halls + 2, plan.Zones.Count);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    public void EveryHallAndTheSupportWingAdjoinTheFoyer(int halls)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = halls });
        string[] arms = plan.Zones.Skip(1).Select(z => z.Id.Value).ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(arms.OrderBy(id => id, StringComparer.Ordinal), PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, "FO"));
        foreach (string arm in arms)
        {
            Assert.Equal(new[] { "FO" }, PlanAssertions.Neighbours(plan, arm));
            Assert.Equal(20.0, plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == arm && w.AdjacentZone?.Value == "FO").Sum(w => w.Length), 9);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WithTwoHallsTheFoyersSouthSideIsItsEntranceFacade()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default);

        WallSurface entrance = Assert.Single(plan.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>(), w => w.Zone.Value == "FO" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors);
        Assert.Equal(OrientationBin.South, Orientation.Bin(entrance.PlanAzimuth));
        Assert.Equal(20.0, entrance.Length, 9);
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.West }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "SV"));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { OrientationBin.East, OrientationBin.North, OrientationBin.South }, PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(plan, "H1"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WithThreeHallsAsWideAsTheFoyerTheFoyerHasNoOutdoorWall()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = 3 }, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Empty(PlanAssertions.OutdoorFacings(result.Value, "FO"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void HallsNarrowerThanTheFoyerLeaveItsCornersOutdoor()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = 3, FoyerWidth = 24.0 }, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        WallSurface[] corners = result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Where(w => w.Zone.Value == "FO" && w.Boundary == BoundaryCondition.Outdoors).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(8, corners.Length);
        Assert.All(corners, w => Assert.Equal(2.0, w.Length, 9));
        Assert.All(corners, w => Assert.Single(w.Windows));
        PlanAssertions.TilesTheFootprint(result.Value);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryOutdoorWallHasOneCentredWindowSizedByItsPreset()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default, Examples);

        Assert.Empty(result.Diagnostics);
        PlanAssertions.HasOneCentredWindowPerOutdoorWall(result.Value, Examples);
        Assert.Equal(10, result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Count(w => w.Windows.Count == 1));
        double halls = 2 * ((2 * 30.0) + 20.0) * 6.0;
        double support = ((2 * 10.0) + 20.0) * 6.0;
        Assert.Equal((0.5 * 20.0 * 6.0) + (0.2 * halls) + (0.1 * support), result.Value.Surfaces.OfType<WallSurface>().Sum(w => w.GlazedArea), 9);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(1, "HallCount must be at least 2 (the foyer serves several distinct activity spaces), got 1.")]
    [InlineData(4, "HallCount must be at most 3 (the support wing takes one of the foyer's four sides, D-100), got 4.")]
    public void HallCountsOutsideTwoToThreeAreErrors(int halls, string message)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = halls }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(message, error.Message);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(0.0, 20.0, 10.0, 20.0, "HallLength")]
    [InlineData(30.0, -20.0, 10.0, 20.0, "HallWidth")]
    [InlineData(30.0, 20.0, double.NaN, 20.0, "SupportLength")]
    [InlineData(30.0, 20.0, 10.0, 0.0, "FoyerWidth")]
    [InlineData(double.PositiveInfinity, 20.0, 10.0, 20.0, "HallLength")]
    public void InvalidParametersAreErrors(double length, double width, double support, double foyer, string name)
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new FoyerHallsParameters(2, length, width, support, foyer, 6.0, 0.0), Examples);

        Assert.False(result.IsSuccess);
        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.StartsWith(name, error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void AHallWiderThanTheFoyerIsAnError()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallWidth = 25.0 }, Examples);

        Diagnostic error = Assert.Single(result.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, error.Code);
        Assert.Equal(
            "HallWidth 25 m is wider than FoyerWidth 20 m; an arm must not be wider than the hub it leaves, or it would overlap its neighbour beyond the hub's corner.",
            error.Message);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void EveryInvalidParameterIsReported()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(new FoyerHallsParameters(4, 0.0, -20.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, double.NaN), Examples);

        Assert.Equal(7, result.Diagnostics.Count(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PresetsHaveOneSlotPerSpaceTypeInOrder()
    {
        Assert.Equal(
            new[] { ("Lobby", SpaceType.Lobby), ("ActivityHall", SpaceType.ActivityHall), ("Service", SpaceType.Service) },
            Examples.Slots.Select(s => (s.Input, s.SpaceType)));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { Examples.Lobby, Examples.ActivityHall, Examples.Service }, Examples.Slots.Select(s => s.Preset));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SwappedPresetsAreAnErrorPerWrongSlot()
    {
        Result<IGeneratedPlan> result = _generator.Generate(
            FoyerHallsParameters.Default,
            new FoyerHallsPresets(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Service, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ActivityHall, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Lobby));

        Assert.Equal(new[] { "Lobby", "Service" }, result.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Code == DiagnosticCodes.PresetSpaceTypeMismatch).Select(d => d.Subject));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void GenerationIsDeterministic()
    {
        Assert.Equal(TextReport.Describe(Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default)), TextReport.Describe(Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void ProvenanceRecordsParametersAndPresets()
    {
        Provenance provenance = Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default).Provenance;

        Assert.Equal(nameof(FoyerHallsGenerator), provenance.Operation);
        Assert.Equal(
            new[]
            {
                "FloorHeight=6", "FoyerWidth=20", "HallCount=2", "HallLength=30", "HallWidth=20", "OrientationDegrees=0",
                "Presets=Lobby:Example Lobby;ActivityHall:Example Activity Hall;Service:Example Service", "SupportLength=10",
            },
            provenance.Parameters.Select(p => $"{p.Key}={p.Value}"));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void CanonicalPlanSnapshot()
    {
        Snapshot.Match(TextReport.Describe(Generate(FoyerHallsParameters.Default)), "foyer-halls-canonical");
    }

    private static Point2[] Rectangle(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY) => Polygon2.Rectangle(minX, minY, maxX, maxY).Outer.ToArray();

    private static Point2[] Outer(IGeneratedPlan plan, string zone) => plan.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == zone).Parts[0].Footprint.Outer.ToArray();

    private IGeneratedPlan Generate(FoyerHallsParameters parameters) => _generator.Generate(parameters, Examples).Value;
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement — create `src/Lod.Generators/FoyerHalls/FoyerHallsGenerator.cs` (full file)**

```csharp
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.HubArms;

namespace Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;

/// <summary>Parameters of the public foyer branching to distinct activity spaces (SYN-TYP-019, ADR-006).</summary>
/// <param name="HallCount">Number of activity halls around the foyer, 2–3.</param>
/// <param name="HallLength">Length of each hall from the foyer to its end, m.</param>
/// <param name="HallWidth">Width of each hall and of the support wing along the foyer's side, m; at most <paramref name="FoyerWidth"/>.</param>
/// <param name="SupportLength">Length of the support wing from the foyer to its end, m.</param>
/// <param name="FoyerWidth">Side of the square foyer, m.</param>
/// <param name="FloorHeight">Floor-to-floor height, m.</param>
/// <param name="OrientationDegrees">Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.</param>
public sealed record FoyerHallsParameters(
    int HallCount,
    double HallLength,
    double HallWidth,
    double SupportLength,
    double FoyerWidth,
    double FloorHeight,
    double OrientationDegrees)
    : PlanParameters(FloorHeight, OrientationDegrees)
{
    /// <summary>The ADR-006 defaults: 2 halls of 30 m × 20 m, a 10 m support wing, a 20 m foyer, 6 m storeys, orientation 0°.</summary>
    public static FoyerHallsParameters Default { get; } = new(2, 30.0, 20.0, 10.0, 20.0, 6.0, 0.0);
}

/// <summary>The program presets of the foyer with halls, one per space type it places (D-061).</summary>
/// <param name="Lobby">Preset of the foyer; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Lobby"/>.</param>
/// <param name="ActivityHall">Preset of the halls; space type <see cref="SpaceType.ActivityHall"/>.</param>
/// <param name="Service">Preset of the support wing; space type <see cref="SpaceType.Service"/>.</param>
public sealed record FoyerHallsPresets(ProgramPreset Lobby, ProgramPreset ActivityHall, ProgramPreset Service) : PlanPresets
{
    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override IReadOnlyList<PresetSlot> Slots => new[]
    {
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Lobby), SpaceType.Lobby, Lobby),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(ActivityHall), SpaceType.ActivityHall, ActivityHall),
        new PresetSlot(nameof(Service), SpaceType.Service, Service),
    };
}

/// <summary>
/// Public foyer branching to distinct activity spaces (SYN-TYP-019, ADR-006), on the shared <see cref="HubArmsLayout"/>: the foyer is the hub, and
/// the activity halls and the support wing are its arms, so the distinct activity spaces and the support rooms lie around the common foyer (R001)
/// and access branches from it: every hall and the support wing adjoins it (R002). Zones are departments (D-097, ADR-014): each hall, and the support
/// rooms together.
/// </summary>
/// <remarks>
/// Arms in the order east, north, west, south: hall 1 east, the support wing north, hall 2 west, and hall 3 south when there are three; with two
/// halls the foyer's south side is its entrance façade, and with three halls as wide as the foyer the foyer has no outdoor wall. Zone IDs: <c>FO</c>,
/// then <c>H1…Hn</c>, then <c>SV</c>; the halls are listed before the support wing, which ADR-006 records as this family's exception to the arm order.
/// </remarks>
public sealed class FoyerHallsGenerator : PlanGenerator<FoyerHallsParameters, FoyerHallsPresets>
{
    /// <summary>The fewest halls: the foyer serves several distinct activity spaces.</summary>
    public const int MinimumHalls = 2;

    /// <summary>The most halls: the support wing takes one of the foyer's four sides (D-100).</summary>
    public const int MaximumHalls = HubArmsLayout.MaximumArms - 1;

    /// <summary>The arm of the support wing: the second in the arm order, plan north.</summary>
    private const int SupportArm = (int)HubSide.North;

    /// <summary>Creates the generator.</summary>
    /// <param name="tolerances">Tolerances.</param>
    public FoyerHallsGenerator(ToleranceSettings tolerances)
        : base(tolerances)
    {
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    public override string Name => nameof(FoyerHallsGenerator);

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<Diagnostic> Validate(FoyerHallsParameters parameters)
    {
        var errors = HubArmsLayout.CountErrors(
            nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.HallCount),
            parameters.HallCount,
            MinimumHalls,
            MaximumHalls,
            "the foyer serves several distinct activity spaces",
            "the support wing takes one of the foyer's four sides").ToList();
        var values = new (string Name, double Value)[]
        {
            (nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.HallLength), parameters.HallLength),
            (nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.HallWidth), parameters.HallWidth),
            (nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.SupportLength), parameters.SupportLength),
            (nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.FoyerWidth), parameters.FoyerWidth),
        };
        var lengths = values
            .Where(v => !IsPositive(v.Value))
            .Select(v => Diagnostic.Error(DiagnosticCodes.InvalidParameter, $"{v.Name} must be positive, got {v.Value}."))
            .ToList();
        if (lengths.Count == 0)
        {
            lengths.AddRange(HubArmsLayout.WidthErrors(nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.HallWidth), parameters.HallWidth, nameof(FoyerHallsParameters.FoyerWidth), parameters.FoyerWidth));
        }

        errors.AddRange(lengths);
        return errors;
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override PlanLayout Layout(FoyerHallsParameters p)
    {
        double[] lengths = Enumerable.Range(0, p.HallCount + 1).Select(i => i == SupportArm ? p.SupportLength : p.HallLength).ToArray();
        var layout = new HubArmsLayout(p.FoyerWidth, p.HallWidth, lengths);
        var zones = new List<PlannedZone> { new(new ZoneId("FO"), "Foyer", SpaceType.Lobby, layout.Hub) };
        HubArm[] halls = layout.Arms.Where(a => (int)a.Side != SupportArm).ToArray();
        for (int i = 0; i < halls.Length; i++)
        {
            zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId($"H{i + 1}"), $"Hall {i + 1}", SpaceType.ActivityHall, halls[i].Footprint));
        }

        zones.Add(new PlannedZone(new ZoneId("SV"), "Support", SpaceType.Service, layout.Arms[SupportArm].Footprint));
        return new PlanLayout(layout.Footprint, zones);
    }

    /// <inheritdoc />
    protected override IEnumerable<KeyValuePair<string, string>> Describe(FoyerHallsParameters p) => new[]
    {
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.HallCount), p.HallCount.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.HallLength), Provenance.Format(p.HallLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.HallWidth), Provenance.Format(p.HallWidth)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.SupportLength), Provenance.Format(p.SupportLength)),
        new KeyValuePair<string, string>(nameof(p.FoyerWidth), Provenance.Format(p.FoyerWidth)),
    };
}
```

- [x] **Step 7: Write the integration tests — create `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/FoyerHallsTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

82 cases: `EveryCombinationValidates` (16), `EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan` (32), `EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostHalls` (32, 2 and 3 halls), and `SemanticMergeKeepsEveryHallApart` (2 and 3 halls: every zone from itself). No production code changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.5 foyer with halls (SYN-TYP-019, ADR-006): two or three activity halls and a support wing around a foyer on the shared hub-and-arms
/// layout, through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most halls.
/// </summary>
public sealed class FoyerHallsTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest and the most halls (ADR-006, D-100).</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { FoyerHallsGenerator.MinimumHalls, FoyerHallsGenerator.MaximumHalls };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostHalls(string simplifier, string aggregator, int halls)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = halls }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [InlineData(2)]
    [InlineData(3)]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsEveryHallApart(int halls)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan(FoyerHallsParameters.Default with { HallCount = halls });

        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType), floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value), floor.Zones.Select(z => Assert.Single(z.SourceZones).Value));
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static FoyerHallsPresets Presets() => new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Lobby, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.ActivityHall, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.Service);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(FoyerHallsParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new FoyerHallsGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? FoyerHallsParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

### Task 7: The *Perimeter Core* precondition on narrow lobes and wings

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/RadialLobesTypologyTests.cs` (Task 2), `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/CareHubTypologyTests.cs` (Task 5)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PerimeterCore(ToleranceSettings, double)`, `DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow` (D-087, ADR-008); the generators of Tasks 2 and 5.
- Produces: `RadialLobesTypologyTests.LobesNotWiderThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrowForPerimeterCore` (9 m lobes) and `CareHubTypologyTests.WingsNotWiderThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrowForPerimeterCore` (3.5 m bedrooms either side of a 2 m corridor, 9 m wings): with the 4.57 m depth, *Perimeter Core* fails with the single error `PlanTooNarrow`. These tests check existing behaviour, so they pass at once (note 5); the generators do not impose the precondition, as the enclosed court does not (S8.3).

- [x] **Step 1: Replace `tests/Lod.Integration.Tests/RadialLobesTypologyTests.cs` (full file)**

One test is added after `PerimeterCoreLeavesACrossShapedCore`; nothing else changes.

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.RadialLobes;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.5 radial lobes (SYN-TYP-010, ADR-006): a cross or a T of dwelling lobes around a point-access core on the shared hub-and-arms layout,
/// through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most lobes.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RadialLobesTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest and the most lobes (ADR-006, D-100).</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { RadialLobesGenerator.MinimumLobes, RadialLobesGenerator.MaximumLobes };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostLobes(string simplifier, string aggregator, int lobes)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(RadialLobesParameters.Default with { LobeCount = lobes }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeUnitesTheDwellingsOfEachLobeAndKeepsTheCore()
    {
        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(new[] { SpaceType.Stair, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit, SpaceType.DwellingUnit }, floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(new[] { "ST" }, floor.Zones[0].SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value));
        for (int lobe = 1; lobe <= 4; lobe++)
        {
            Zone dwellings = floor.Zones[lobe];
            Assert.Equal(new[] { $"L{lobe}-U1", $"L{lobe}-U2" }, dwellings.SourceZones.Select(z => z.Value));
            Assert.Equal(120.0, dwellings.FloorArea, 6);
            Assert.Empty(dwellings.Parts[0].Footprint.Holes);
        }
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreLeavesACrossShapedCore()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Assert.Equal(
            "P-North-1,P-North-2,P-North-3,P-East-1,P-East-2,P-East-3,P-South-1,P-South-2,P-South-3,P-West-1,P-West-2,P-West-3,CORE",
            string.Join(",", floor.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value)));
        Zone core = floor.Zones.Single(z => z.Id.Value == "CORE");
        double arm = 12.0 - (2 * 4.57);
        Assert.Equal((2 * (32.0 - (2 * 4.57)) * arm) - (arm * arm), core.FloorArea, 6);
        Assert.Equal(9, core.SourceZones.Count);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void LobesNotWiderThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrowForPerimeterCore()
    {
        // 9 m lobes are narrower than 2 × 4.57 m (D-087, ADR-008); the generator does not impose the simplifier's precondition.
        Result<IFloor> floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan(RadialLobesParameters.Default with { LobeWidth = 9.0 }));

        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, Assert.Single(floor.Diagnostics).Code);
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static RadialLobesPresets Presets() => new(ExampleResidentialPresets.DwellingUnit, ExampleResidentialPresets.Stair);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(RadialLobesParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new RadialLobesGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? RadialLobesParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Lod.Core.Buildings;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Plans;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Core.Simplification;
using Lod.Core.Validation;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Xunit;

namespace Lod.Integration.Tests;

/// <summary>
/// The S8.5 care hub (SYN-TYP-018, ADR-006): two to four wings of bedrooms off a corridor around a communal hub on the shared hub-and-arms layout,
/// with 0.8 m hub corners beside the 14.4 m wings, through every simplifier and aggregator, canonical and placed, with the fewest and the most wings.
/// </summary>
public sealed class CareHubTypologyTests
{
    private static readonly string[] Simplifiers = { "NoSimplification", "SemanticMerge", "PerimeterCore", "SingleZonePerFloor" };
    private static readonly string[] Aggregators = { "Stack", "StackedFloorZoneMultiplier", "SingleZonePerFloorType", "SingleZoneBuilding" };

    /// <summary>Rotations (degrees) and translations (m) applied by <see cref="PlanTransform"/>.</summary>
    private static readonly double[][] Placements = { new[] { 30.0, 5.3, -2.1 }, new[] { 131.4, 15.08, -153.9 } };

    /// <summary>The fewest and the most wings (ADR-006, D-100).</summary>
    private static readonly int[] Counts = { CareHubGenerator.MinimumWings, CareHubGenerator.MaximumWings };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> Matrix() =>
        from simplifier in Simplifiers
        from aggregator in Aggregators
        select new object[] { simplifier, aggregator };

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> PlacedMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from placement in Enumerable.Range(0, Placements.Length)
        select combination.Append(placement).ToArray();

    public static IEnumerable<object[]> CountMatrix() =>
        from combination in Matrix()
        from count in Counts
        select combination.Append(count).ToArray();

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(Matrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidates(string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(PlacedMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesOnARotatedAndTranslatedPlan(string simplifier, string aggregator, int placement)
    {
        double[] p = Placements[placement];

        AssertValidates(PlanTransform.Apply(Plan(), p[0], p[1], p[2]).Value, simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Theory]
    [MemberData(nameof(CountMatrix))]
    public void EveryCombinationValidatesWithTheFewestAndTheMostWings(string simplifier, string aggregator, int wings)
    {
        AssertValidates(Plan(CareHubParameters.Default with { WingCount = wings }), simplifier, aggregator);
    }

    [Fact]
    public void SemanticMergeKeepsEveryDepartmentBecauseNoTwoOfOneTypeTouch()
    {
        IGeneratedPlan plan = Plan();

        IFloor floor = new SemanticMerge(Pipeline.Tolerances).Simplify(plan).Value;

        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType), floor.Zones.Select(z => z.SpaceType));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => z.Id.Value), floor.Zones.Select(z => Assert.Single(z.SourceZones).Value));
        Assert.Equal(plan.Zones.Select(z => System.Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 6)), floor.Zones.Select(z => System.Math.Round(z.FloorArea, 6)));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void PerimeterCoreGivesEachHubCornerItsOwnSmallPerimeterZone()
    {
        IFloor floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan()).Value;

        Zone[] corners = floor.Zones.Where(z => z.SourceZones.Count == 1 && z.SourceZones[0].Value == "HUB" && z.Id.Value != "CORE" && z.FloorArea < 5.0).ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(6, corners.Length);
        Assert.All(corners, z => Assert.Equal(0.8 * 4.57, z.FloorArea, 6));
    }

    [Fact]
    public void WingsNotWiderThanTwiceThePerimeterDepthAreTooNarrowForPerimeterCore()
    {
        // 3.5 m bedrooms either side of a 2 m corridor make 9 m wings, narrower than 2 × 4.57 m (D-087, ADR-008).
        Result<IFloor> floor = new PerimeterCore(Pipeline.Tolerances, 4.57).Simplify(Plan(CareHubParameters.Default with { BedroomDepth = 3.5, CorridorWidth = 2.0 }));

        Assert.Equal(DiagnosticCodes.PlanTooNarrow, Assert.Single(floor.Diagnostics).Code);
    }

    private static void AssertValidates(IGeneratedPlan plan, string simplifier, string aggregator)
    {
        Result<IFloor> floor = Pipeline.Simplifier(simplifier).Simplify(plan);
        Assert.True(floor.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", floor.Diagnostics));
        FloorEntry[] entries = { new(floor.Value, 1), new(floor.Value, 3), new(floor.Value, 1) };

        Result<IGeneratedBuilding> building = Pipeline.Aggregator(aggregator).Aggregate(entries);
        Assert.True(building.IsSuccess, string.Join("; ", building.Diagnostics));
        ValidationReport report = new BuildingValidator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Validate(building.Value);

        Assert.True(report.Passed, report.Describe());
        Assert.DoesNotContain(building.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Warning);
    }

    private static CareHubPresets Presets() => new(ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareBedroom, ExampleNonResidentialPresets.CareCommunal, ExampleResidentialPresets.Corridor);

    /// <summary>The canonical plan: ADR-006 defaults and the example presets, or other parameters.</summary>
    private static IGeneratedPlan Plan(CareHubParameters? parameters = null) =>
        new CareHubGenerator(Pipeline.Tolerances).Generate(parameters ?? CareHubParameters.Default, Presets()).Value;
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Drawing;
using Grasshopper.Kernel;
using Lod.Core.Common;
using Lod.Core.Model;
using Lod.Core.Programs;
using Lod.Generators.BranchingMall;
using Lod.Generators.Cafeteria;
using Lod.Generators.CareHub;
using Lod.Generators.EnclosedCourt;
using Lod.Generators.FoyerHalls;
using Lod.Generators.GalleryBar;
using Lod.Generators.OfficePlate;
using Lod.Generators.OpenCourt;
using Lod.Generators.PointPlate;
using Lod.Generators.RadialLobes;
using Lod.Generators.StairBayBar;
using Lod.Generators.StairPair;
using Lod.Generators.TerraceRow;
using Lod.Generators.WingedBand;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Goo;
using Lod.Grasshopper.Parameters;

namespace Lod.Grasshopper.Components;

/// <summary>
/// Shared inputs and output of the typology plan generator components (ADR-006): number inputs with the family's defaults, one required preset input
/// per space type (D-061, D-065), the Preview Location last (D-062), and one Plan output.
/// </summary>
public abstract class TypologyGeneratorComponent : GH_Component
{
    protected const string FloorHeightDescription = "Floor-to-floor height, m.";

    protected const string OrientationDescription = "Clockwise rotation of plan north from true north, degrees.";

    protected const string Metres = " All lengths in metres regardless of model units.";

    protected TypologyGeneratorComponent(string name, string nickname, string description)
        : base(name, nickname, description + Metres, ComponentCategories.Category, ComponentCategories.Generate)
    {
    }

    protected override Bitmap? Icon => IconLibrary.For(this);

    protected override void RegisterOutputParams(GH_OutputParamManager pManager)
    {
        pManager.AddParameter(new PlanParameter(), "Plan", "Plan", "The detailed plan.", GH_ParamAccess.item);
    }

    /// <summary>Adds the required preset input of one space type.</summary>
    protected static void AddPreset(GH_InputParamManager pManager, string name, string nickname, SpaceType spaceType, string role) =>
        pManager.AddParameter(new ProgramPresetParameter(), name, nickname, $"Program preset of {role}; space type {spaceType}.", GH_ParamAccess.item);

    /// <summary>Reads <paramref name="count"/> number inputs from <paramref name="first"/>; <c>false</c> when one is missing.</summary>
    protected static bool TryNumbers(IGH_DataAccess DA, int first, int count, out double[] values)
    {
        values = new double[count];
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (!DA.GetData(first + i, ref values[i]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>Reads a preset input; <c>null</c> when it is unconnected, which Grasshopper reports as a missing input (D-065).</summary>
    protected static ProgramPreset? Preset(IGH_DataAccess DA, int index)
    {
        ProgramPresetGoo? goo = null;
        return DA.GetData(index, ref goo) && goo is not null ? goo.Value : null;
    }

    /// <summary>Reports the generator's diagnostics and outputs the plan with this component's preview location.</summary>
    protected void Output(IGH_DataAccess DA, Result<IGeneratedPlan> result)
    {
        IGeneratedPlan? plan = ComponentSupport.Unwrap(this, result);
        if (plan is not null)
        {
            DA.SetData(0, new PlanGoo(plan, ComponentSupport.PreviewLocation(this, DA)));
        }
    }
}

public sealed class StairBayBarComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairBayBarComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Bay Bar",
            "SBar",
            "Repeated stair bays in a linear bar (HDHM-TYP-001, ADR-006): bays of dwelling | stair | dwelling through the full depth, each stair serving the two dwellings beside it; no corridor. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("911c3204-42bb-4023-bdb7-2931ea1caad3");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairBayBarParameters d = StairBayBarParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddIntegerParameter("Bay Count", "BC", "Number of stair bays along the bar; at least 2.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.BayCount);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Bar depth, which every dwelling and stair spans, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of each stair along the bar, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stairs");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int bays = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref bays) || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 5, out double[] v) || Preset(DA, 6) is not { } dwellingUnit || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } stair)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new StairBayBarParameters(bays, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4]);
        Output(DA, new StairBayBarGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new StairBayBarPresets(dwellingUnit, stair)));
    }
}

public sealed class StairPairComponent : TypologyGeneratorComponent
{
    public StairPairComponent()
        : base(
            "Stair Pair",
            "SPair",
            "One stair between a dwelling pair (HDHM-TYP-008, ADR-006): one stair bay of the Stair Bay Bar as its own building, the stair serving both dwellings. One zone per dwelling unit.")
    {
    }

    public override Guid ComponentGuid => new("5669d39b-45e6-47ea-9250-f2304d005335");

    protected override void RegisterInputParams(GH_InputParamManager pManager)
    {
        StairPairParameters d = StairPairParameters.Default;
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Unit Width", "UW", "Width of each dwelling, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.UnitWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Depth", "D", "Plate depth, which both dwellings and the stair span, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.Depth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Stair Width", "SW", "Width of the stair between the dwellings, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.StairWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Dwelling Unit", "DU", SpaceType.DwellingUnit, "the two dwellings");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Stair", "St", SpaceType.Stair, "the stair");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Hall Width", "HW", "Width of each hall and of the support wing along the foyer's side, m; at most Foyer Width.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.HallWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Support Length", "SL", "Length of the support wing from the foyer to its end, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.SupportLength);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Foyer Width", "FW", "Side of the square foyer, m.", GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FoyerWidth);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Floor Height", "FH", FloorHeightDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.FloorHeight);
        pManager.AddNumberParameter("Orientation", "O", OrientationDescription, GH_ParamAccess.item, d.OrientationDegrees);
        AddPreset(pManager, "Lobby", "Lo", SpaceType.Lobby, "the foyer");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Activity Hall", "AH", SpaceType.ActivityHall, "the activity halls");
        AddPreset(pManager, "Service", "Sv", SpaceType.Service, "the support wing");
        pManager.AddParameter(ComponentSupport.PreviewLocationParameter());
    }

    protected override void SolveInstance(IGH_DataAccess DA)
    {
        int halls = 0;
        if (!DA.GetData(0, ref halls)
            || !TryNumbers(DA, 1, 6, out double[] v)
            || Preset(DA, 7) is not { } lobby
            || Preset(DA, 8) is not { } activityHall
            || Preset(DA, 9) is not { } service)
        {
            return;
        }

        var parameters = new FoyerHallsParameters(halls, v[0], v[1], v[2], v[3], v[4], v[5]);
        Output(DA, new FoyerHallsGenerator(ToleranceSettings.Default).Generate(parameters, new FoyerHallsPresets(lobby, activityHall, service)));
    }
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Replace `scripts/make_icons.py` (full file)**

The four motifs are added after `small_building`, and their entries after *Cafeteria* in the Generate group; every other motif and entry is unchanged.

```python
"""Draws the BEMGen Grasshopper icons (D-063).

Every icon is drawn at 96 x 96 pixels on a transparent background and downsampled to 24 x 24 with LANCZOS, then written to
src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons/<Name>.png, where <Name> is the class name of the component, parameter, or assembly info that shows it.
Each icon is a dark glyph plus the accent colour of its toolbar panel; a parameter uses the panel of the component that makes its object.
Shapes only, no fonts, so the output does not depend on installed fonts.

Run from the repository root:  python scripts/make_icons.py
Requires Python 3.10+ and Pillow. Generated with Pillow 12.3.0; the same Pillow version gives the same pixels.
The script stops with an error when an icon has no class in src/Lod.Grasshopper or a class has no icon.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
SOURCE = ROOT / "src" / "Lod.Grasshopper"
OUTPUT = SOURCE / "Icons"

CANVAS = 96
SIZE = 24
LINE = 8  # 2 px after downsampling

DARK = (38, 38, 38, 255)
WHITE = (255, 255, 255, 255)

# One accent per toolbar panel (ComponentCategories).
INFO = (96, 125, 139, 255)
PROGRAM = (230, 140, 30, 255)
GENERATE = (46, 158, 79, 255)
SIMPLIFY = (47, 123, 216, 255)
AGGREGATE = (138, 79, 200, 255)
CONVERT = (208, 69, 58, 255)
INSPECT = (24, 150, 150, 255)

Colour = tuple[int, int, int, int]
Draw = ImageDraw.ImageDraw
Motif = Callable[[Draw, Colour], None]
Painter = Callable[[Image.Image, Colour], None]


def box(d: Draw, xy: tuple[float, float, float, float], fill=None, outline=DARK, width: int = LINE) -> None:
    d.rectangle(xy, fill=fill, outline=outline, width=width)


def line(d: Draw, points: list[tuple[float, float]], width: int = LINE, fill=DARK) -> None:
    d.line(points, fill=fill, width=width, joint="curve")


def arrow_head(d: Draw, tip: tuple[float, float], direction: str, size: float, fill=DARK) -> None:
    x, y = tip
    if direction == "right":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x - size, y + size)], fill=fill)
    elif direction == "down":
        d.polygon([(x, y), (x - size, y - size), (x + size, y - size)], fill=fill)


# --- motifs: each draws inside the 96 x 96 canvas -------------------------------------------------------------


def info(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 90, 90), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.rectangle((42, 40, 54, 74), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((42, 20, 54, 32), fill=WHITE)


def assembly(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A stepped block: the building at three levels of detail.
    box(d, (8, 52, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 30, 60, 56), fill=accent)
    box(d, (8, 8, 36, 34), fill=accent)


def schedule(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A 24-hour profile as bars on a time axis.
    heights = [16, 48, 72, 36, 60, 76, 24]
    for i, h in enumerate(heights):
        x = 12 + i * 12
        d.rectangle((x, 84 - h, x + 7, 83), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((4, 84, 92, 91), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((4, 4, 11, 91), fill=DARK)


def load(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    bolt = [(58, 4), (18, 54), (44, 54), (34, 92), (78, 38), (52, 38), (64, 4)]
    d.polygon(bolt, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)


def preset_tag(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    tag = [(8, 30), (30, 8), (88, 8), (88, 88), (30, 88), (8, 66)]
    d.polygon(tag, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((24, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
    line(d, [(52, 34), (76, 34)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 48), (76, 48)], width=6)
    line(d, [(52, 62), (76, 62)], width=6)


def house(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(48, 6), (90, 44), (6, 44)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    box(d, (16, 42, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 60, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(16, 48), (60, 48)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (78, 48), "right", 12)


def stair(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    steps = [(6, 90), (6, 70), (28, 70), (28, 50), (50, 50), (50, 30), (72, 30), (72, 10), (90, 10), (90, 90)]
    d.polygon(steps, fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def office(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # An office block in elevation with a grid of windows.
    box(d, (16, 6, 80, 90), fill=accent)
    for y in (18, 38, 58):
        for x in (26, 52):
            d.rectangle((x, y, x + 16, y + 12), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((40, 76, 56, 90), fill=DARK)


def core(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A service core: a box with two lift doors and an up and down arrow.
    box(d, (8, 8, 88, 88), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((20, 34, 42, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((54, 34, 76, 80), fill=DARK)
    d.polygon([(48, 12), (38, 26), (58, 26)], fill=DARK)


def retail(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A shopping bag with its handle.
    d.arc((30, 6, 66, 46), start=180, end=360, fill=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.polygon([(14, 30), (82, 30), (90, 90), (6, 90)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)


def mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A branching mall in plan: shop blocks (accent) along a T of public routes (white).
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((40, 8, 56, 88), fill=WHITE)
    d.rectangle((8, 40, 40, 56), fill=WHITE)
    for a, b in [((40, 4), (40, 40)), ((56, 4), (56, 92)), ((40, 56), (40, 92)), ((4, 40), (40, 40)), ((4, 56), (40, 56))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def kitchen(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cooking pot with a lid and two handles.
    box(d, (16, 40, 80, 86), fill=accent)
    line(d, [(4, 50), (16, 50)])
    line(d, [(80, 50), (92, 50)])
    d.rectangle((12, 28, 84, 38), fill=DARK)

def dining(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

def operating_theatre(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A medical cross.
    d.polygon(
        [(34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 34), (90, 34), (90, 62), (62, 62), (62, 90), (34, 90), (34, 62), (6, 62), (6, 34), (34, 34)],
        fill=accent,

    )


def clean_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a white (clean) route and an arrow.
    box(d, (4, 30, 92, 66), fill=accent)
    d.rectangle((10, 42, 70, 54), fill=WHITE)

def dirty_corridor(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A corridor with a hatched (dirty) route and an arrow.

def clinical_support(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Shelves of supplies beside a small medical cross.

def care_bedroom(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    line(d, [(62, 10), (62, 88)], width=4)

def activity_hall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

def service_rooms(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

def gallery_bar(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A bar of dwellings (accent) between two end stairs (dark); the gallery along it is dashed, as it is not modelled.

    d.rectangle((8, 38, 18, 72), fill=DARK)

    for x in (36, 54):

def terrace_row(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    for left, right in houses:
        d.rectangle((left + 10, 68, right - 10, 88), fill=DARK)


def point_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A square plate: four dwellings (accent) in a pinwheel around one central core (dark) without façades.

    for a, b in [((6, 36), (60, 36)), ((60, 6), (60, 60)), ((36, 60), (90, 60)), ((36, 36), (36, 90))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def winged_band(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    d.polygon(
        [(4, 90), (92, 90), (92, 8), (70, 8), (70, 62), (59, 62), (59, 8), (37, 8), (37, 62), (26, 62), (26, 8), (4, 8)],

def open_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A U in plan: a north wing (top) across the full width and two side wings, around a court open to the south (bottom); stairs dark.

def enclosed_court(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    for y in (7, 69):
        for x in (24, 66):

    for left in (7, 69):
        d.rectangle((left, 45, left + 20, 51), fill=DARK)


def office_plate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangular plate: one office work area (accent) all around a central service core (dark).

    d.rectangle((32, 40, 64, 56), fill=DARK)


def cafeteria(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A rectangle: the kitchen and servery along the north edge (dark) and the dining field (accent) with tables.
    box(d, (4, 10, 92, 86), fill=accent)

    for y in (48, 68):
        for x in (20, 44, 68):
            d.ellipse((x, y, x + 10, y + 10), fill=DARK)


def transform_plan(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A footprint outline and the same footprint rotated and moved, joined by a turning arrow.

    d.polygon([(66, 12), (92, 28), (76, 56), (50, 40)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)

def z0(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Many cells: every unit its own zone.
    box(d, (4, 4, 91, 91), fill=accent)
    for k in (32, 60):
        line(d, [(k, 4), (k, 91)])

def z1(d: Draw, accent) -> None:

    box(d, (4, 56, 92, 86), fill=accent)
    box(d, (4, 36, 92, 60))


def z2(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # Perimeter ring split on the corner bisectors, plus a core.
    box(d, (4, 4, 92, 92))

    for a, b in [((4, 4), (30, 30)), ((92, 4), (66, 30)), ((4, 92), (30, 66)), ((92, 92), (66, 66))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def z3(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (6, 16, 90, 80), fill=accent)


def slab(d: Draw, accent, y: float, height: float = 16, left: float = 6, right: float = 90) -> None:
    box(d, (left, y, right, y + height), fill=accent, width=6)


def stack(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    for y in (8, 36, 64):
        slab(d, accent, y, 24)


def multiplier(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    slab(d, accent, 6, 24, 6, 90)
    line(d, [(18, 44), (50, 84)], width=10)
    line(d, [(50, 44), (18, 84)], width=10)
    for y in (44, 58, 72):
        d.rectangle((62, y, 90, y + 8), fill=DARK)


def single_zone_per_floor_type(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # One zone per floor entry: a one-storey zone below a three-storey zone.
    box(d, (24, 66, 71, 91), fill=accent)
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 58), fill=accent)


def single_zone_building(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    box(d, (24, 4, 71, 91), fill=accent)


def convert(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.polygon([(40, 44), (90, 44), (90, 92), (40, 92)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(6, 14), (44, 14), (64, 34), (64, 56)], width=10)
    arrow_head(d, (64, 74), "down", 16)


def inspect(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((6, 6, 64, 64), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    d.ellipse((18, 18, 38, 38), fill=WHITE)
    line(d, [(56, 56), (88, 88)], width=16)


def mini_grid(d: Draw, origin: tuple[float, float], size: float, cells: int, accent) -> None:
    x0, y0 = origin
    box(d, (x0, y0, x0 + size, y0 + size), fill=accent, width=6)
    step = size / cells
    for k in range(1, cells):
        line(d, [(x0 + k * step, y0), (x0 + k * step, y0 + size)], width=4)
        line(d, [(x0, y0 + k * step), (x0 + size, y0 + k * step)], width=4)


def map_source_target(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    mini_grid(d, (4, 4), 40, 2, accent)
    box(d, (52, 52, 92, 92), fill=accent, width=6)
    line(d, [(32, 56), (32, 72), (40, 72)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (52, 72), "right", 12)
    line(d, [(56, 26), (72, 26), (72, 36)], width=8)
    arrow_head(d, (72, 50), "down", 12)


def validate(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    d.ellipse((4, 4, 92, 92), fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=LINE)
    line(d, [(24, 50), (42, 68), (74, 30)], width=14, fill=WHITE)


# Parameter motifs: small versions of their object's motif, drawn inside the hexagon (x and y in 20..76).

def radial_lobes(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A cross in plan: four lobes (accent) on the sides of a square core (dark), each lobe split along its axis into two dwellings.
    d.polygon(
        [(32, 4), (64, 4), (64, 32), (92, 32), (92, 64), (64, 64), (64, 92), (32, 92), (32, 64), (4, 64), (4, 32), (32, 32)],
        fill=accent,
        outline=DARK,
        width=6,
    )
    d.rectangle((32, 32, 64, 64), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((64, 48), (92, 48)), ((48, 4), (48, 32)), ((4, 48), (32, 48)), ((48, 64), (48, 92))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)


def branching_mall(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A T in plan: shop wings (accent) along a mall (dark) that branches from a central court into three branches, anchors at the branch ends.
    d.polygon([(4, 50), (34, 50), (34, 6), (62, 6), (62, 50), (92, 50), (92, 82), (4, 82)], fill=accent, outline=DARK, width=6)
    d.rectangle((18, 62, 78, 70), fill=DARK)
    d.rectangle((44, 20, 52, 70), fill=DARK)
    for a, b in [((18, 50), (18, 82)), ((78, 50), (78, 82)), ((34, 20), (62, 20))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=6)


def care_hub(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A communal hub (accent, a table in its middle) with three narrower bedroom wings (accent), each with a corridor (dark) along its axis.
    for xy in [(66, 49, 92, 75), (4, 49, 30, 75), (35, 6, 61, 42)]:
        box(d, xy, fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (30, 42, 66, 82), fill=accent, width=6)
    for a, b in [((66, 62), (92, 62)), ((4, 62), (30, 62)), ((48, 6), (48, 42))]:
        line(d, [a, b], width=4)
    d.ellipse((40, 54, 56, 70), fill=DARK)


def foyer_halls(d: Draw, accent) -> None:
    # A foyer (white) with two large halls (accent) east and west, the support wing (dark) north, and its entrance from the south.
    box(d, (4, 40, 34, 74), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (62, 40, 92, 74), fill=accent, width=6)
    box(d, (34, 18, 62, 40), fill=DARK, width=6)
    box(d, (34, 40, 62, 74), fill=WHITE, width=6)
    d.polygon([(48, 78), (40, 90), (56, 90)], fill=DARK)


# --- icons ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def component(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A component: its motif at full size."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        motif(ImageDraw.Draw(image), accent)

    return paint


def parameter(motif: Motif) -> Painter:
    """A parameter: a small version of its object's motif inside a hexagon (the Grasshopper parameter shape)."""

    def paint(image: Image.Image, accent: Colour) -> None:
        d = ImageDraw.Draw(image)
        d.polygon([(24, 4), (72, 4), (94, 48), (72, 92), (24, 92), (2, 48)], fill=WHITE, outline=DARK, width=4)
        motif(d, accent)

    return paint


ICONS: dict[str, tuple[Colour, Painter]] = {
    "BemGenAssemblyInfo": (INFO, component(assembly)),
    "BemGenInfoComponent": (INFO, component(info)),
    "ScheduleComponent": (PROGRAM, component(schedule)),
    "LoadComponent": (PROGRAM, component(load)),
    "ProgramPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(preset_tag)),
    "DwellingUnitPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(house)),
    "CorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(corridor)),
    "StairPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(stair)),
    "OfficePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(office)),
    "CorePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(core)),
    "RetailPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(retail)),
    "MallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(mall)),
    "KitchenPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(kitchen)),
    "DiningPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dining)),
    "OperatingTheatrePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(operating_theatre)),
    "CleanCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clean_corridor)),
    "DirtyCorridorPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(dirty_corridor)),
    "ClinicalSupportPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(clinical_support)),
    "CareBedroomPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_bedroom)),
    "CareCommunalPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(care_communal)),
    "LobbyPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(lobby)),
    "ActivityHallPresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(activity_hall)),
    "ServicePresetComponent": (PROGRAM, component(service_rooms)),
    "LinearPlanGeneratorComponent": (GENERATE, component(linear_generator)),
    "StairBayBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_bay_bar)),
    "StairPairComponent": (GENERATE, component(stair_pair)),
    "GalleryBarComponent": (GENERATE, component(gallery_bar)),
    "TerraceRowComponent": (GENERATE, component(terrace_row)),
    "PointPlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(point_plate)),
    "WingedBandComponent": (GENERATE, component(winged_band)),
    "OpenCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(open_court)),
    "EnclosedCourtComponent": (GENERATE, component(enclosed_court)),
    "OfficePlateComponent": (GENERATE, component(office_plate)),
    "CafeteriaComponent": (GENERATE, component(cafeteria)),
    "RadialLobesComponent": (GENERATE, component(radial_lobes)),
    "BranchingMallComponent": (GENERATE, component(branching_mall)),
    "CareHubComponent": (GENERATE, component(care_hub)),
    "FoyerHallsComponent": (GENERATE, component(foyer_halls)),
    "TransformPlanComponent": (GENERATE, component(transform_plan)),
    "NoSimplificationComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z0)),
    "SemanticMergeComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z1)),
    "PerimeterCoreComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z2)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorComponent": (SIMPLIFY, component(z3)),
    "StackFloorsComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(stack)),
    "StackedFloorZoneMultiplierComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(multiplier)),
    "SingleZonePerFloorTypeComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_per_floor_type)),
    "SingleZoneBuildingComponent": (AGGREGATE, component(single_zone_building)),
    "Convert2BemComponent": (CONVERT, component(convert)),
    "InspectComponent": (INSPECT, component(inspect)),
    "MapSourceToTargetComponent": (INSPECT, component(map_source_target)),
    "ValidateComponent": (INSPECT, component(validate)),
    "ScheduleParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_schedule)),
    "LoadParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_load)),
    "ProgramPresetParameter": (PROGRAM, parameter(small_preset)),
    "PlanParameter": (GENERATE, parameter(small_plan)),
    "FloorParameter": (SIMPLIFY, parameter(small_floor)),
    "BuildingParameter": (AGGREGATE, parameter(small_building)),
}


def render(name: str) -> Image.Image:
    accent, paint = ICONS[name]
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (CANVAS, CANVAS), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    paint(image, accent)
    return image.resize((SIZE, SIZE), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)


def classes_with_icons() -> set[str]:
    """Concrete classes that show an icon: components, parameters, and the assembly info."""
    pattern = re.compile(r"public sealed class (\w+) : (\w+)")
    names = set()
    for path in SOURCE.rglob("*.cs"):
        if "obj" in path.parts or "bin" in path.parts:
            continue
        for name, _ in pattern.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            if name.endswith(("Component", "Parameter")) or name == "BemGenAssemblyInfo":
                names.add(name)
    return names


def main() -> int:
    expected = classes_with_icons()
    missing = sorted(expected - ICONS.keys())
    unknown = sorted(ICONS.keys() - expected)
    if missing or unknown:
        print(f"Classes without an icon: {missing}; icons without a class: {unknown}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    OUTPUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stale = sorted(p.name for p in OUTPUT.glob("*.png") if p.stem not in ICONS)
    if stale:
        print(f"Icons without a class in {OUTPUT}: {stale}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    for name in sorted(ICONS):
        render(name).save(OUTPUT / f"{name}.png", optimize=False)
    print(f"Wrote {len(ICONS)} icons to {OUTPUT.relative_to(ROOT)}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

Draw a contact sheet of all icons at 8× (nearest neighbour) into the build folder, which git ignores:

```bash
python - <<'EOF'
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
icons = sorted(Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/Icons").glob("*.png"))
cell = 24 * 8 + 16
sheet = Image.new("RGBA", (6 * cell, -(-len(icons) // 6) * cell), (235, 235, 235, 255))
for i, path in enumerate(icons):
    big = Image.open(path).convert("RGBA").resize((24 * 8, 24 * 8), Image.Resampling.NEAREST)
    sheet.alpha_composite(big, ((i % 6) * cell + 8, (i // 6) * cell + 8))
Path("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
sheet.save("src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
print(len(icons), "icons drawn on src/Lod.Grasshopper/bin/icon-sheet.png")
EOF
```

Every icon after each of them moves one place on, the 53 icons of S8.4 keep their motifs, and the 57 icons fill nine rows of six and three icons of a tenth.

This document describes each step, the rules it applies, where results are validated, and the Grasshopper components that expose it. The domain types are described in [domain-model.md](domain-model.md), the validation checks in [validation.md](validation.md), and the `Convert2BEM` output layout in [convert2bem.md](convert2bem.md).
